                                       Part 1

【当季新题】2026 年 05-08 月 Part 1 正式题库和解析

Tidiness

Question 1:
Do you like to keep things tidy?

 Useful phrases:
 clean environment 整洁环境
 feel more comfortable 感觉更舒服
 stay organised 保持有条理
Sample answer:
Yes, I do like to keep things tidy, because a clean environment helps me relax and concentrate better. When my
desk or room gets messy, I start to feel distracted and uncomfortable quite quickly. Staying organised also saves
time, since I can always find what I need without searching around.
Question 2:
Did you use to keep your room tidy as a child?
 Useful phrases:
 messy room 杂乱的房间
 remind me constantly 经常提醒我
 clean things up 收拾整理
Sample answer:
Not really, my room used to be quite messy when I was a child, especially after playing with toys or reading
comics. My parents had to remind me constantly to clean things up before bedtime. As I grew older, though, I
slowly became more organised and started enjoying a tidy space.

Mirrors

Question 1:
Do you like looking at yourself in the mirror? How often?

 Useful phrases:
 check my appearance 检查仪表
 before going out 出门之前
 not too often 不太频繁
Sample answer:
I look at myself in the mirror mostly to check my appearance before going out or after getting dressed. It’s
more of a practical habit than something I spend a lot of time on. Honestly, I don’t do it too often unless I need
to make sure I look presentable.
Question 2:
Have you ever bought mirrors?
 Useful phrases:
 room decoration 房间装饰
 simple design 简约设计
 make the space brighter 让空间更明亮
Sample answer:
Yes, I’ve bought mirrors before, mainly for room decoration rather than just practical use. I usually prefer
mirrors with a simple design because they match most furniture styles easily. A good mirror can also make the
space brighter and feel more open, which is something I really like.

6
Question 3
Do you usually take a mirror with you?

 Useful phrases:
 compact mirror 小镜子

 check my appearance 检查仪表

 fit in my bag 放进包里
Sample answer:
No, I don’t usually take a mirror with me because I can easily use my phone camera if necessary. Sometimes I
carry a compact mirror when I travel or attend important events, just to check my appearance quickly. Since it’s
small and can fit in my bag easily, it’s convenient without taking up much space.
Question 4
Would you use mirrors to decorate your room?

 Useful phrases:
 modern style 现代风格

 make the room brighter 让房间更明亮

 create more space 营造更大空间感
Sample answer:
Yes, I would use mirrors to decorate my room because they match a modern style quite well. Mirrors can make
the room brighter by reflecting light, especially in smaller spaces. They also create more space visually, so the
room feels more open and comfortable.

Teachers

Question 1:
Do you have a favorite teacher?

 Useful phrases:
 supportive attitude 支持性的态度

 explain clearly 讲解清楚

 leave a strong impression 留下深刻印象
Sample answer:
Yes, I do have a favorite teacher from high school who taught English. She always had a supportive attitude and
could explain clearly even when the topic was difficult. Her classes left a strong impression on me, and she
made learning feel much more enjoyable.
Question 2:
Do you want to be a teacher in the future?

 Useful phrases:
 share knowledge 分享知识

 help others improve 帮助别人提升

 meaningful career 有意义的职业
Sample answer:
Yes, I think being a teacher could be a meaningful career because I enjoy sharing knowledge and
communicating with people. Helping others improve their skills or confidence sounds very rewarding to me.
Although teaching can be stressful at times, I still find the idea attractive and worthwhile.
Question 3
Do you have a teacher from your past that you still remember?

 Useful phrases:
 encouraging attitude 鼓励性的态度

 make lessons interesting 让课堂有趣

 unforgettable influence 难忘的影响

7
Sample answer:
Yes, I still remember one of my middle school teachers because she had such an encouraging attitude toward
students. She always found ways to make lessons interesting, even when the topic was difficult or boring. Her
support had an unforgettable influence on me, and it changed the way I viewed studying.
Question 4
Are you still in touch with your primary school teachers?

 Useful phrases:
 keep in touch 保持联系
 social media apps 社交媒体软件
 special memories 特别回忆
Sample answer:
I’m still in touch with a few of my primary school teachers through social media apps and holiday messages.
Although we don’t talk very often, I still appreciate the support they gave me when I was younger. Seeing their
messages sometimes brings back special memories from childhood.

Social media

Question 1:
Have you ever posted anything on social media?

 Useful phrases:
 share daily life 分享日常生活
 post photos 发照片
 keep in touch 保持联系
Sample answer:
Yes, I’ve posted things on social media before, mostly photos or short updates about daily life. I think it’s a
convenient way to keep in touch with friends and share memorable moments. However, I don’t post very often
because I prefer to keep some parts of my life private.
Question 2:
When did you start using social media?
 Useful phrases:
 secondary school 中学时期
 stay connected 保持联系
 online platforms 网络平台
Sample answer:
I started using social media when I was in secondary school, mainly because many classmates were already
using online platforms every day. At first, I only used it to stay connected and chat with friends after class. Over
time, it became a common part of daily life for entertainment and information as well.
Question 3
Do you think you spend too much time on social media?
 Useful phrases:
 scroll through feeds 刷动态
 waste time 浪费时间
 limit screen time 限制屏幕时间
Sample answer:
Yes, I think I probably spend too much time on social media, especially when I start scrolling through feeds
without noticing the time. Although it helps me relax and stay updated, it can also waste time if I’m not careful.
That’s why I’ve been trying to limit screen time and focus more on real-life activities recently.

8
Question 4
Do your friends use social media?

 Useful phrases:
 share updates 分享动态

 online platforms 网络平台

 daily habit 日常习惯
Sample answer:
Yes, almost all of my friends use social media every day because it has become part of their daily habit. They
often share updates, photos, or short videos on different online platforms. It’s an easy way for everyone to stay
connected even when we are busy.

Dream and ambition

Question 1:
What was your childhood dream?

 Useful phrases:
 dream job 梦想职业

 look exciting 看起来很酷

 change over time 随时间改变
Sample answer:
When I was a child, my dream job was to become a pilot because flying looked exciting and adventurous to me.
I used to imagine travelling around the world and seeing different places every day. Of course, that dream
changed over time as I discovered other interests and career options.
Question 2:
Are you the kind of person who sticks to dreams?

 Useful phrases:
 stay determined 保持坚定

 give up easily 轻易放弃

 work toward goals 朝目标努力
Sample answer:
Yes, I think I’m the kind of person who tries to stick to dreams once I decide something is important to me.
Even when things become difficult, I prefer to stay determined rather than give up easily. Of course, I may
adjust my plans sometimes, but I still keep working toward goals step by step.
Question 3
What is your dream job?

 Useful phrases:
 creative work 创造性工作

 make a difference 带来改变

 personal interest 个人兴趣
Sample answer:
My dream job is something related to education and creative work, because I enjoy sharing ideas and helping
people improve. I’d love to do work that can make a difference in students’ learning experiences while also
matching my personal interest. For me, a meaningful job is more important than simply earning a high salary.
Question 4
Do you think you are an ambitious person?

 Useful phrases:
 set goals 设定目标

 work hard 努力奋斗

 improve myself 提升自己

9
Sample answer:
Yes, I think I’m quite an ambitious person because I like to set goals and challenge myself step by step. Once I
decide what I want, I’m usually willing to work hard and stay focused for a long time. At the same time, I also
try to improve myself gradually instead of comparing myself with others all the time.

Music

Question 1:
Do you prefer sad or happy music?

 Useful phrases:
 cheerful songs 欢快歌曲

 lift my mood 提升心情

 emotional lyrics 富有感情的歌词
Sample answer:
I generally prefer happy music because cheerful songs can lift my mood and make me feel more energetic. That
said, I sometimes listen to sad music when I want to relax or think quietly. Music with emotional lyrics can be
comforting too, depending on how I feel that day.
Question 2:
Does happy music make you feel more excited?

 Useful phrases:
 upbeat rhythm 欢快节奏

 boost my energy 提升精力

 positive mood 积极情绪
Sample answer:
Yes, happy music definitely makes me feel more excited, especially songs with an upbeat rhythm and strong
beats. Listening to that kind of music can boost my energy when I feel tired or stressed. It also helps me stay in
a positive mood and makes daily activities feel more enjoyable.

Science

Question 1:
Do you like science?

 Useful phrases:
 understand the world 了解世界

 scientific discoveries 科学发现

 interesting subject 有趣的学科
Sample answer:
Yes, I do like science because it helps people understand the world in a logical and practical way. I’m always
amazed by scientific discoveries that improve daily life or solve problems. Even though some topics can be
difficult, I still think science is a very interesting subject.
Question 2:
When did you start to learn about science?

 Useful phrases:
 primary school 小学时期

 simple experiments 简单实验

 science classes 科学课
Sample answer:
I started learning about science in primary school through basic science classes and simple experiments. At
that age, I was curious about how things worked, so these lessons felt exciting rather than difficult. Those early
experiences helped me become more interested in science later on.

10
Question 3
Which science subject is interesting to you?

 Useful phrases:
 human body 人体

 natural phenomena 自然现象

 everyday life 日常生活
Sample answer:
Biology is probably the science subject that interests me the most because I’m curious about the human body
and how living things function. I also enjoy learning about natural phenomena like evolution and ecosystems.
What makes biology appealing is that it connects closely with everyday life and health.
Question 4
What kinds of interesting things have you done with science?

 Useful phrases:
 school experiments 学校实验

 hands-on activities 动手活动

 learn through practice 通过实践学习
Sample answer:
I’ve done some interesting school experiments in science classes, like building simple circuits and observing
chemical reactions. Those hands-on activities made learning much more enjoyable than only reading
textbooks. I think science becomes more interesting when people can learn through practice instead of just
memorising facts.

Watch

Question 1:
Do you wear a watch?

 Useful phrases:
 check the time 看时间

 simple design 简约设计

 daily accessory 日常配饰
Sample answer:
Yes, I usually wear a watch because it’s convenient to check the time quickly without using my phone. I prefer
watches with a simple design since they match most of my clothes easily. For me, a watch is not only practical
but also a nice daily accessory.
Question 2:
Have you ever got a watch as a gift?

 Useful phrases:
 birthday present 生日礼物

 special meaning 特殊意义

 wear it often 经常佩戴
Sample answer:
Yes, I once got a watch as a birthday present from a family member, and it meant a lot to me. Even though it
wasn’t very expensive, it had special meaning because it reminded me of that important moment. I still wear it
often when I want something simple and classic.
Question 3
Why do some people wear expensive watches?

 Useful phrases:
 status symbol 身份象征

 fashion item 时尚单品

 personal taste 个人品味

11
Sample answer:
I think some people wear expensive watches because they see them as a status symbol or a fashion item. A
high-end watch can also reflect someone’s personal taste and lifestyle. Besides telling time, these watches
often make people feel more confident and professional.
Question 4
Do you think it is important to wear a watch? Why?

 Useful phrases:
 manage time 管理时间

 professional image 职业形象

 depend on phones 依赖手机
Sample answer:
Yes, I think wearing a watch is still important, even though many people depend on phones to check the time
nowadays. A watch helps people manage time more easily and can also create a more professional image in
formal situations. Besides that, checking a watch feels quicker and less distracting than constantly using a
phone.

Websites

Question 1:
What kinds of websites do you often visit?

 Useful phrases:
 search for information 查找信息

 online learning 在线学习

 spend free time 打发空闲时间
Sample answer:
I often visit websites related to online learning, news, and entertainment. Sometimes I search for information
for work or study, while other times I just watch videos to spend free time. Useful and well-organised websites
usually keep me interested longer.
Question 2:
What is your favourite website?

 Useful phrases:
 search for ideas 寻找灵感

 useful content 有用内容

 spend spare time 打发空闲时间
Sample answer:
My favorite website is probably a video-sharing platform where I can search for ideas and learn new things
easily. It has a lot of useful content about study skills, travel, and daily life, so I rarely get bored. I also use it to
spend spare time and relax after work or study.
Question 3
Are there any changes to the websites you often visit?

 Useful phrases:
 spend more time 花更多时间

 short videos 短视频

 learning platforms 学习平台
Sample answer:
Yes, the websites I use have changed quite a lot over the years. In the past I mostly visited entertainment sites,
but now I spend more time on learning platforms and work-related pages. I still watch short videos sometimes,
but I try to use the internet more productively now.

12
Question 4
What kinds of websites are popular in your country?

 Useful phrases:
 social media platforms 社交媒体平台

 online shopping 网上购物

 short-video apps 短视频软件
Sample answer:
In my country, social media platforms and online shopping websites are extremely popular among people of all
ages. Many people also spend a lot of time on short-video apps because they are entertaining and easy to use.
Besides that, learning websites and news platforms have become more common in recent years as people care
more about self-improvement.

Parks

Question 1:
Did you like going to parks as a child?

 Useful phrases:
 fresh air 新鲜空气

 take a walk 散步

 peaceful environment 宁静环境
Sample answer:
Yes, I enjoy going to parks whenever I have free time, especially in the evening or on weekends. Walking in a
peaceful environment with fresh air helps me relax and clear my mind after a busy day. I think parks are
important because they give people space to slow down and rest.
Question 2:
Do you still like going to parks now?

 Useful phrases:
 take a break 休息一下

 natural surroundings 自然环境

 walk around 四处走走
Sample answer:
Yes, I still enjoy going to parks because they give me a chance to take a break from busy city life. Being
surrounded by trees and fresh air always makes me feel calmer and more relaxed. Sometimes I simply walk
around there with friends or listen to music alone.
Question 3
Would you like to see more parks in your city?

 Useful phrases:
 green spaces 绿化空间

 improve air quality 改善空气质量

 outdoor activities 户外活动
Sample answer:
Yes, I’d definitely like to see more parks in my city because green spaces make urban life feel less stressful.
Parks can also improve air quality and give people more chances to do outdoor activities. I think having more
natural areas would make the city healthier and more pleasant to live in.
Question 4
Are there any parks you want to go to in the future?

 Useful phrases:
 national park 国家公园

 beautiful scenery 美丽风景

 peaceful atmosphere 宁静氛围

13
Sample answer:
Yes, I’d really like to visit a famous national park in the future because I enjoy being close to nature. I’ve seen
photos of its beautiful scenery, including mountains, lakes, and forests, and the place looks incredibly relaxing.
Spending time in such a peaceful atmosphere would be a great way to escape city life for a while.

Cars

Question 1:
Did you enjoy traveling by car when you were a kid?

 Useful phrases:
 drive independently 独立开车
 save time 节省时间
 public transport 公共交通
Sample answer:
Yes, I’d like to learn how to drive because it would make travelling much more convenient. Being able to drive
independently could save time, especially when public transport is crowded or unavailable. I also think driving
is a useful life skill for the future.
Question 2:
What types of cars do you like?
 Useful phrases:
 modern design 现代设计
 comfortable interior 舒适内饰
 fuel-efficient 省油的
Sample answer:
I like cars with a modern design and a comfortable interior, because I care more about practicality than speed.
Fuel-efficient cars are especially attractive to me since they save money and are better for the environment. I
also prefer models that feel safe and easy to drive in daily life.
Question 3
Do you prefer to be a driver or a passenger?
 Useful phrases:
 sit back 放松坐着
 avoid traffic stress 避免堵车压力
 enjoy the ride 享受旅程
Sample answer:
I prefer being a passenger because I can sit back and relax instead of focusing on the road all the time. Driving
in heavy traffic can be stressful and tiring, especially in big cities. As a passenger, I can enjoy the ride, listen to
music, or look at the scenery outside.
Question 4
What do you usually do when there is a traffic jam?
 Useful phrases:
 listen to music 听音乐
 waste time 浪费时间
 stay patient 保持耐心
Sample answer:
When there is a traffic jam, I usually listen to music or check messages on my phone to pass the time. Traffic
jams can be frustrating because they make people waste time and feel stressed. Still, I try to stay patient
instead of getting angry, since there’s usually nothing I can do to change the situation quickly.

14
Shopping

Question 1:
Do you like shopping?

 Useful phrases:
 buy useful things 买实用的东西
 window shopping 逛街不买
 good deals 优惠折扣
Sample answer:
Yes, I quite enjoy shopping, especially when I’m looking for clothes or small items for daily life. Sometimes I just
go window shopping with friends without planning to buy anything. Finding good deals or something I really
like always makes the experience more enjoyable.
Question 2:
How often do you go shopping?
 Useful phrases:
 once in a while 偶尔
 buy necessities 购买必需品
 online shopping 网上购物
Sample answer:
I don’t go shopping very often, maybe just once in a while when I need to buy necessities like clothes or daily
supplies. Most of the time I prefer online shopping because it’s faster and more convenient. Still, I sometimes
enjoy going to shopping malls with friends for fun.
Question 3
Do you prefer online shopping or in-store shopping?
 Useful phrases:
 compare prices 比较价格
 try things on 试穿
 convenient choice 方便的选择
Sample answer:
I prefer online shopping because it’s a more convenient choice and saves a lot of time. I can easily compare
prices and look through many products without leaving home. However, for clothes or shoes, I still like in-store
shopping so I can try things on before buying them.
Question 4
Have you ever returned anything you bought online?
 Useful phrases:
 wrong size 尺码不对
 different from the pictures 和图片不一样
 return process 退货流程
Sample answer:
Yes, I’ve returned things I bought online a few times, mostly because the size was wrong or the product looked
different from the pictures. Luckily, the return process was usually simple and didn’t take too long. Even so,
those experiences made me more careful about checking reviews before ordering something.

15
Clothing

Question 1:
What kind of clothes do you like to wear?

 Useful phrases:
 casual clothes 休闲服装
 feel comfortable 感觉舒服
 simple style 简约风格
Sample answer:
I usually like wearing casual clothes because they make me feel comfortable and relaxed throughout the day.
Most of the time, I prefer a simple style with neutral colours instead of anything too flashy. For me, comfort is
more important than following fashion trends all the time.
Question 2:
Do you prefer to wear comfortable and casual clothes or smart clothes?
 Useful phrases:
 casual outfit 休闲穿搭
 feel relaxed 感觉放松
 formal occasions 正式场合
Sample answer:
I definitely prefer comfortable and casual clothes because they make me feel relaxed and free to move around
easily. Most of the time, a simple casual outfit is enough for my daily life and work. Of course, I still wear smart
clothes for formal occasions when I need to look more professional.
Question 3
Do you like wearing T-shirts?
 Useful phrases:
 comfortable clothing 舒适的衣服
 casual style 休闲风格
 hot weather 炎热天气
Sample answer:
Yes, I enjoy wearing T-shirts because they are simple, comfortable, and suitable for daily life. I especially like
them in hot weather since they feel light and easy to move around in. Besides that, T-shirts match a casual style
that works well for school, shopping, or relaxing with friends.
Question 4

Dreams

Question 1:
Can you remember the dreams you had?

 Useful phrases:
 vivid dreams 生动的梦
 fade quickly 很快消失
 strange details 奇怪的细节
Sample answer:
I can sometimes remember my dreams, especially when they are vivid or emotional. However, most of the
time they fade quickly after I wake up, and only a few strange details stay in my mind. I usually forget them
completely by the time I start my day.
Question 2:
Do you share your dreams with others?
 Useful phrases:
 close friends 亲密朋友
 keep it private 保持私密
 feel understood 被理解
Sample answer:
I don’t usually share my dreams with many people, only with close friends or family members. Some dreams
feel too personal, so I prefer to keep them private unless I really trust someone. When I do share them, it
makes me feel understood and supported.
Question 3
Do you think dreams have special meanings?
 Useful phrases:
 reflect emotions 反映情绪
 daily stress 日常压力
 not take seriously 不太当真
Sample answer:
I think dreams sometimes reflect emotions or daily stress rather than having special meanings. When I’m
tired or worried, my dreams tend to be more intense or strange. Most of the time, though, I don’t take them
too seriously and just see them as part of the brain resting.
Question 4
Do you want to make your dreams come true?
 Useful phrases:
 come true（梦想 / 梦境）成真
 in real life 在现实生活中
 look forward to 期待，盼望

Sample answer:
Yes, I sometimes wish my dreams could come true. It feels amazing when something I dreamed about happens
in real life. Even if not all of them do, those vivid dreams still make life more interesting and mysterious. I
always look forward to seeing if any of my dreams will repeat or come true one day.

Typing

Question 1:

Question 4

 typing speed 打字速度
 daily use 日常使用
Sample answer:
I improve my typing mainly by practicing regularly through daily use. Writing emails, assignments, and
messages naturally increases my typing speed. Over time, typing has become automatic without me focusing
on it.

Childhood activities

What are your favourite activities?

 spend time alone 独处一会

Question 4

                                     Part 2&3

【当季新题】2026 年 05-08 月 Part 2&3 正式题库和解析

People【人】

想从事医疗行业的人

 Describe a person you know who would like to choose a career in the medical field (e.g. a doctor, a nurse).
 You should say:
 When you knew him or her
 When he or she started to think about that
 What he or she would like to do
 And explain why he or she would like to choose this career

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“人物类 + 职业规划类”话题，要求你描述一个想从事医疗行业的人。需要说清：你什么时候认识他或她、
他或她什么时候开始有这个想法、未来想做什么，以及为什么会选择这个职业。整体以过去时和现在时结合为主。
重点在于职业动机和个人特点。
思路方面
这里可以选择朋友、同学或亲戚。思路上可以先说清你是什么时候认识这个人的；然后要说清他或她什么时候开始
想进入医疗行业；接着可以重点说具体想从事什么工作，比如医生或护士；最后解释为什么他或她会选择这个职
业。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个人是谁以及你什么时候认识他或她；
第二段：描述他或她什么时候开始考虑医疗行业以及想做什么；
第三段：解释他或她为什么选择这个职业，并表达你的看法。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The person I want to talk about is my classmate. 我想说的人是我的同学。
描述感受  I met her in high school. 我是在高中认识她的。
      She has always been interested in science. 她一直对科学感兴趣。
      She is patient and responsible. 她很有耐心也很负责。
      Many people think she is suitable for this career. 很多人觉得她适合这个职业。
      She started thinking about this career a few years ago. 她几年前开始考虑这个职业。
      She would like to become a doctor. 她想成为一名医生。
      She spends a lot of time studying biology. 她花很多时间学习生物。
      She also enjoys helping other people. 她也喜欢帮助别人。
      She hopes to work in a large hospital one day. 她希望未来在大医院工作。
      I admire her determination. 我佩服她的决心。
      This career requires a lot of hard work. 这个职业需要很多努力。
      I think she has the right personality for it. 我觉得她有适合这个职业的性格。
      Her goal sounds meaningful and valuable. 她的目标听起来很有意义。
      That is why I support her decision. 这就是我支持她决定的原因。

35
Sample Answer ：
A person I know who would like to choose a career in the medical field is one of my classmates from high
school. I met her when we were in the same science class, and over time I noticed that she was very interested
in subjects related to biology and health. She was also known as a patient and responsible person, so many
classmates thought she would be suitable for medical work in the future.
She started thinking seriously about this career a few years ago when one of her family members became ill
and spent a long time in the hospital. During that period, she saw how doctors and nurses worked hard to help
patients every day. Since then, she has wanted to become a doctor herself. She now spends a lot of time
studying biology and other science subjects because she hopes to enter medical school one day.
I think she wants to choose this career mainly because she genuinely enjoys helping other people. She once
told me that she would like to make patients feel safer and more comfortable during difficult times. In addition,
she enjoys solving problems and learning scientific knowledge, which are both important in the medical field.
Personally, I really admire her determination because becoming a doctor requires many years of hard work and
study. I believe she has the right personality for this career, and I truly support her decision because it is both
meaningful and valuable.

Part 3
Question 1: Do you think being a doctor is easy or difficult?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“学习时间长”“工作压力大”“责任重”三个方面展开，说明医生职业的难度不仅来
 自知识，还来自责任感。
Sample Answer:
I think being a doctor is very difficult. Doctors need to study for many years and learn a large amount of
medical knowledge. They must also continue learning because medicine changes over time.
Their work is stressful because they deal with sick people and make important decisions. Sometimes they need
to work long hours or night shifts. A small mistake can seriously affect a patient’s health.
So, being a doctor requires knowledge, patience, and a strong sense of responsibility, which makes it a
challenging job.

Question 2: Do you think learning biology is interesting for children?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“了解自然”“满足好奇心”“实践性强”三个角度展开，解释为什么生物对儿童来说
 容易产生兴趣。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I think learning biology is interesting for children. Biology helps children understand animals, plants, and
the human body, which are all closely connected to daily life. Children are naturally curious about nature, so
they often enjoy these topics.
Biology can also include experiments and outdoor activities, which make learning more active and fun.
Compared with subjects that require a lot of memorization, biology feels more practical and visual.
So, biology is an interesting subject because it combines knowledge, curiosity, and real-life experience.

在成功公司工作的人

 Describe a person who works in a successful company
 You should say:
 Who he or she is
 What he or she does in the company
 What business the company does
 And explain why you think it is a successful company

36
教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“人物类 + 工作类”话题，要求你描述一位在成功公司工作的人。需要说清：这个人是谁、他或她在公司里
做什么、公司从事什么业务，以及你为什么觉得这家公司很成功。整体以现在时为主。重点在于人物工作内容和公
司成功原因。
思路方面
这里可以选择亲戚、朋友或前同事。思路上可以先说清这个人的身份和你们的关系；然后要说清他或她在公司中的
具体工作；接着介绍公司的业务内容；最后重点说你为什么认为这家公司成功，比如规模大、服务好或发展快。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个人是谁以及他或她在哪里工作；
第二段：描述他或她的工作内容和公司业务；
第三段：解释为什么你认为这家公司成功，并表达你的感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The person I want to talk about is my cousin. 我想说的人是我的表哥。
描述感受  He works for a large technology company. 他在一家大型科技公司工作。
      I have known about his job for several years. 我了解他的工作已经好几年了。
      The company is very popular in my country. 这家公司在我国家很有名。
      Many young people want to work there. 很多年轻人都想在那里工作。
      He works in the marketing department. 他在市场部门工作。
      His job is to communicate with clients and promote products. 他的工作是和客户沟通并推广产品。
      The company mainly develops technology products. 这家公司主要开发科技产品。
      It provides services to many customers. 它为很多顾客提供服务。
      The company is growing very quickly. 这家公司发展得很快。
      I think the company is well managed. 我觉得这家公司管理得很好。
      It has a strong reputation. 它有很强的口碑。
      Many people trust its products and services. 很多人信任它的产品和服务。
      I admire the working environment there. 我很欣赏那里的工作环境。
      That is why I think it is a successful company. 这就是我认为它成功的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A person who works in a successful company is my cousin. He works for Tencent
, which is one of the largest technology companies in China. I have known about his work for several years
because we often talk during family gatherings, and he likes sharing stories about his job.
He works in the marketing department, and his main responsibility is to communicate with clients and help
promote the company’s online services and products. Sometimes he also works with other teams to plan
business activities and improve customer experience. According to him, the company mainly focuses on
technology, social media, online entertainment, and digital services. Many people use its apps and platforms
every day, so the company has a very large customer base.
I think this is a successful company for several reasons. First, it has a strong reputation and is well known both
in China and internationally. Second, its products are widely used and trusted by millions of people. In addition,
the company keeps developing new ideas and improving its services, which helps it stay competitive. My
cousin also told me that the working environment is professional and organized, and employees have many
opportunities to grow. Because of all these reasons, I believe it is a very successful company, and I feel proud
that my cousin works there.

37
Part 3
Question 1: Do you think governments should provide financial support to companies?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以采取“有条件支持”的平衡观点。
Sample Answer:
Yes, I think governments should provide financial support to companies in some situations. For example, small
businesses may need help during economic difficulties or public emergencies. This support can protect jobs
and help the economy stay stable.
However, support should be fair and carefully controlled. Governments should help companies that truly need
support and contribute to society.
So, financial support can be useful, but it should be given responsibly and with clear rules.

Question 2: Do you think companies should donate money to help society?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“社会责任”“企业形象”“资源能力”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I think companies should donate money to help society when they are able to do so. Large companies
often have more resources, so their support can help schools, hospitals, or people in need.
Donations also improve a company’s public image and show social responsibility. However, companies should
donate in a sincere and transparent way, not only for advertising.
So, helping society through donations is positive for both communities and companies.

Question 3: Do you think customer satisfaction is important for a company?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“长期发展”“客户忠诚”“公司口碑”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, customer satisfaction is very important for a company. Happy customers are more likely to buy products
again and recommend the company to others. This helps businesses grow in the long term.
Customer satisfaction also improves a company’s reputation. Today, online reviews spread very quickly, so bad
service can damage a company’s image.
So, keeping customers satisfied is important for both business success and public trust.

发小

 Describe a friend from your childhood
 You should say:
 Who he or she is
 Where and how you met each other
 What you often did together
 And explain what made you like him or her

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“人物类 + 童年回忆类”话题，要求你描述一位童年时期的朋友。需要说清：这个朋友是谁、你们在哪里以
及如何认识、你们经常一起做什么，以及你为什么喜欢他或她。整体以过去时为主，最后可以适当用现在时表达现
在的感受。重点在于共同经历和朋友的性格特点。
思路方面
这里可以选择小学同学、邻居或亲戚家的孩子。思路上可以先说清你们是如何认识的；然后要说清你们小时候经常
做的事情；接着可以重点说这个朋友的性格，比如友善、幽默或乐于帮助别人；最后总结你为什么喜欢他或她。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个朋友是谁，以及你们如何认识；
第二段：描述你们小时候经常一起做什么；
第三段：解释你为什么喜欢他或她，并总结感受。

38
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The friend I want to talk about is named Jack. 我想说的朋友叫 Jack。
描述感受  We met in primary school. 我们是在小学认识的。
      We were classmates for many years. 我们当了很多年同学。
      He lived near my neighborhood. 他住在我家附近。
      We became close friends quickly. 我们很快成了好朋友。
      We often played games after school. 我们放学后经常一起玩游戏。
      Sometimes we rode bicycles together. 有时候我们一起骑自行车。
      We also did homework together. 我们也会一起写作业。
      We spent most weekends together. 我们很多周末都一起度过。
      We shared many happy memories. 我们有很多快乐回忆。
      He was very friendly and funny. 他非常友好也很幽默。
      He always made people feel comfortable. 他总能让别人感到轻松。
      I could talk to him about anything. 我什么都可以和他说。
      He was always willing to help me. 他总愿意帮助我。
      That is why I liked him so much. 这就是我很喜欢他的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A friend from my childhood that I still remember clearly is a boy named Jack. We met in primary school when
we were about eight years old. At that time, we were in the same class, and we quickly became friends
because we both enjoyed similar activities. He also lived quite close to my neighborhood, so we often spent
time together after school.
When we were children, we did many things together. After finishing homework, we often played games in the
park or rode bicycles around our neighborhood. During weekends, we sometimes watched cartoons or talked
about toys and school life. We also studied together before exams, although most of the time we were simply
having fun and enjoying each other’s company. Those small activities became some of my happiest childhood
memories.
What made me like him most was his personality. He was very friendly, funny, and easy to talk to. Whenever I
felt upset or nervous, he could always make me laugh. He was also willing to help others, even with small
things. I remember once when I forgot my homework, he stayed with me after class and helped me finish it.
Being with him always made me feel relaxed and comfortable.
Although we do not meet very often now, I still think of him whenever I remember my childhood. That is why
he remains one of the most important childhood friends in my memory.

Part 3
Question 1: Do you still keep in touch with your friends from childhood? Why or why not?

 解题思路：个人经历类问题。可以从“距离变化”“共同经历”“生活节奏”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I still keep in touch with some childhood friends. We shared many memories when growing up, so the
friendship feels special and comfortable.
Although we are now busy with work or study, we still chat online or meet during holidays. However, I have
also lost contact with some friends because we moved to different places and have different lifestyles now.
So, childhood friendships can last a long time, but keeping them requires effort and communication.

39
Question 2: How important is childhood friendship to children?
 解题思路：影响类问题。可以从“情感支持”“社交能力”“成长记忆”展开。

Sample Answer:
Childhood friendship is very important to children. Friends give emotional support and help children feel happy
and accepted. Playing and learning together also improve communication skills.
Friendships teach children how to share, cooperate, and solve small conflicts. These experiences are useful for
future relationships. Childhood friends also create special memories that many people remember for a long
time.
So, childhood friendship plays an important role in emotional and social development.

Question 3: What do you think of communicating via social media?
 解题思路：：观点类问题。可以从“便利性”和“情感局限”两个角度展开。

Sample Answer:
I think communicating through social media is very convenient. People can stay connected with friends and
family anytime, even when they live far away. It also allows people to share information quickly.
However, social media communication may feel less personal than face-to-face conversation. Messages can
sometimes be misunderstood because people cannot see facial expressions or hear tone of voice.
So, social media is useful for communication, but real-life interaction is still important.

Question 4: Do you think online communication through social media will replace face-to-face
communication?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以明确“不会完全取代”，再解释原因。
Sample Answer:
No, I don’t think social media communication will completely replace face-to-face communication. Online
communication is fast and convenient, especially for people who live far away.
However, face-to-face communication is more personal and emotional. People can see facial expressions, body
language, and feelings more clearly in real life. This helps build stronger relationships and avoid
misunderstanding.
So, social media is useful, but direct communication will still remain important in people’s lives.

喜欢在家或花园种植物的人

 Describe a person who loves to grow plants (e.g. vegetables, flowers) at home or in the garden
 You should say:
 Who this person is
 What plants he or she grows
 How he or she grows the plants
 And explain why he or she loves growing plants

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“人物类 + 兴趣爱好类”话题，要求你描述一个喜欢种植物的人。需要说清：这个人是谁、他或她种什么植
物、如何种植这些植物，以及为什么他或她喜欢种植物。整体以现在时为主。重点在于种植习惯和背后的原因。
思路方面
这里可以选择家人、邻居或朋友。思路上可以先说清这个人的身份；然后要说清他或她种植的植物类型；接着可以
重点说种植方式，比如阳台、花园或日常照顾过程；最后说明他或她为什么喜欢种植物，比如放松、健康或热爱自
然。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个人是谁；
第二段：描述他或她种什么植物以及如何种植；
第三段：解释他或她为什么喜欢种植物，并表达你的感受。

40
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  e person I want to talk about is my grandfather. 我想说的人是我爷爷。
描述感受  He enjoys spending time in the garden. 他喜欢待在花园里。
      He has grown plants for many years. 他种植物很多年了。
      Gardening is one of his favorite hobbies. 园艺是他最喜欢的爱好之一。
      He knows a lot about plants. 他懂很多植物知识。
      He grows vegetables and flowers. 他种蔬菜和花。
      He waters the plants every morning. 他每天早上给植物浇水。
      He also uses natural fertilizer. 他还会使用天然肥料。
      He spends a lot of time taking care of the garden. 他花很多时间照顾花园。
      The plants always look healthy and fresh. 这些植物总是看起来健康又新鲜。
      Growing plants helps him relax. 种植物能让他放松。
      He enjoys being close to nature. 他喜欢亲近自然。
      It gives him a sense of achievement. 这给他带来成就感。
      I admire his patience and care. 我很佩服他的耐心和细心。
      That is why I think he truly loves gardening. 这就是我认为他真正热爱园艺的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A person who loves growing plants is my grandfather. He has been interested in gardening for many years, and
it has become one of his favorite hobbies. Behind his house, there is a small garden where he spends a lot of
his free time every day.
He mainly grows vegetables and flowers. For vegetables, he grows tomatoes, peppers, and green onions, while
for flowers, he likes growing roses and sunflowers. Every morning, he waters the plants carefully and checks
whether they are healthy. He also removes weeds and sometimes uses natural fertilizer to help the plants grow
better. During different seasons, he changes the types of plants in the garden and learns new ways to take care
of them. Because of his effort, the garden always looks colorful and fresh.
I think he loves growing plants because it helps him relax and feel close to nature. Whenever he talks about his
garden, he looks happy and excited. He also enjoys seeing the plants grow little by little, which gives him a
strong sense of achievement. In addition, he likes sharing vegetables and flowers with neighbors and family
members, which makes him feel useful and connected to others. I really admire his patience and care, because
gardening requires a lot of time and attention. That is why I believe he truly loves growing plants and enjoys
every moment he spends in the garden.

Part 3
Question 1: What are the advantages of growing vegetables or flowers at home?

 解题思路：优势类问题。可以从“放松身心”“健康”“美化环境”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Growing vegetables or flowers at home has many advantages. First, it helps people relax and reduce stress.
Taking care of plants can make people feel calm and happy.
Growing vegetables is also healthy because people can eat fresh food they grow themselves. Flowers make the
home look more beautiful and comfortable.
In addition, gardening teaches patience and responsibility. So, growing plants at home is good for both physical
and mental well-being.

Question 2: Do many people grow vegetables or flowers at home in your country?

 解题思路：现象类问题。可以从“城市与农村差异”“兴趣”“生活方式”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, some people in my country grow vegetables or flowers at home, especially older people and families with
gardens or balconies. Flowers are more common in cities because they make homes look beautiful and
relaxing.

41
In rural areas, people are more likely to grow vegetables because they have more space. Growing plants is also
seen as a healthy and enjoyable hobby.
So, while not everyone grows plants at home, it is still a popular activity in many families.

Question 3: Is it easy to grow plants at home?
 解题思路：评估类问题。可以给出“简单但需要耐心”的平衡观点。

Sample Answer:
Growing plants at home can be easy if people choose simple plants. Some flowers and vegetables only need
sunlight, water, and basic care.
However, certain plants are harder to grow because they need special temperatures or regular attention. Busy
people may also forget to take care of them.
So, growing plants at home is possible for most people, but success depends on time, patience, and the type of
plant.

Question 4: Why do some people like to grow plants?
 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“放松”“美化环境”“健康生活”三个角度展开。

Sample Answer:
People like to grow plants because it helps them relax and feel closer to nature. Taking care of plants can
reduce stress and improve mood.
Plants also make homes more beautiful and comfortable. Some people grow vegetables because they enjoy
fresh and healthy food. In addition, gardening gives people a sense of achievement when plants grow well.
So, growing plants is popular because it benefits both mental health and daily life.

拥有成功商业的人

 Describe a person you know who has a successful business
 You should say:
 Who this person is
 How you got to know him or her
 Why and how he or she started the business
 What business he or she does
 And explain why you think the business is successful

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“人物类 + 商业类”话题，要求你描述一个经营成功生意的人。需要说清：这个人是谁、你是怎么认识他或
她的、他或她为什么以及如何开始创业、从事什么生意，以及你为什么认为这门生意是成功的。整体以过去时和现
在时结合为主。重点在于创业原因、经营方式和成功原因。
思路方面
这里可以选择亲戚、朋友或邻居。思路上可以先说清你和这个人的关系；然后要说清他或她为什么开始创业；接着
重点可以放在生意内容以及经营方式；最后说明为什么你觉得这门生意很成功，比如顾客多、口碑好或发展稳定。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个人是谁，以及你是如何认识他或她的；
第二段：描述他或她为什么创业以及具体经营什么；
第三段：解释为什么你认为这门生意成功，并表达你的看法。

42
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The person I want to talk about is my uncle. 我想说的人是我叔叔。
描述感受  I have known him since childhood. 我从小就认识他。
      He owns a small business. 他经营一家小生意。
      He started the business several years ago. 他几年前开始创业。
      At first, the business was quite small. 一开始生意规模很小。
      He runs a local restaurant. 他经营一家本地餐馆。
      He started the business because he enjoys cooking. 他创业是因为喜欢做饭。
      He worked very hard in the beginning. 一开始他工作非常努力。
      He pays attention to customer service. 他很重视顾客服务。
      The restaurant is always busy. 这家餐馆总是很忙。
      描述感受
      I think he is very determined. 我觉得他很有决心。
      The business has a good reputation. 这门生意口碑很好。
      Many customers return regularly. 很多顾客都会再来。
      I admire his hard work and patience. 我佩服他的努力和耐心。
      That is why I think his business is successful. 这就是我认为他生意成功的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A person I know who has a successful business is my uncle. I have known him since childhood because he is a
close family member, and over the years I have watched his business grow step by step. He owns a local
restaurant in our city, and it has become quite popular among local people.
My uncle started this business several years ago because he has always enjoyed cooking. Before opening his
own restaurant, he worked in other restaurants and learned a lot about food and customer service. At first, his
business was very small, and he had to do many things by himself, including cooking, cleaning, and talking with
customers. However, he worked very hard and slowly improved the restaurant. He focused on serving fresh
food and treating customers politely, which helped him build a good reputation.
Now the restaurant is usually busy, especially during weekends and holidays. Many customers come back
regularly, and some even recommend the place to their friends. I think the business is successful not only
because it makes money, but also because people genuinely like it and trust it. My uncle is also very patient
and responsible, and he never stops improving his service. I really admire his hard work and determination.
Watching him succeed taught me that running a business requires both effort and good communication skills.
That is why I believe his business is truly successful.

Part 3
Question 1: Why do some people start their own business?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“自由”“赚钱机会”“个人兴趣”展开。

Sample Answer:
Some people start their own business because they want more freedom. They prefer making their own
decisions instead of following company rules all the time.
Others believe running a business can bring better income and personal success. Some people also want to
turn their hobbies or ideas into work.
Although starting a business is risky, it can give people a strong sense of achievement. So, independence,
interest, and financial goals are common reasons for becoming entrepreneurs.

43
Question 2: Should governments provide financial support to start-ups?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“创新”“就业”“风险控制”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, governments should provide some financial support to start-ups. New businesses can create jobs and bring
new ideas to society. Many small companies have potential but lack money in the beginning.
Government support can help them survive difficult early stages. However, support should be carefully
managed, and companies should meet clear standards.
So, helping start-ups can support innovation and economic growth when done responsibly.

Question 3: Do most people prefer shopping at big stores or small stores?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以给出“大店更普遍”的观点，再补充小店的优势。

Sample Answer:
I think most people prefer shopping at big stores because they offer more choices and lower prices. People can
buy many things in one place, which saves time and feels convenient.
However, some people still like small stores because the service is more personal and friendly. Small shops may
also sell local or unique products.
So, big stores are more popular overall, but small stores still have their own advantages.

Question 4: What makes a business successful?
 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“产品质量”“客户服务”“管理能力”展开。

Sample Answer:
A successful business usually provides good products or services. If customers are satisfied, they are more likely
to return and recommend the business to others.
Good management is also important. Business owners need clear plans and responsible workers. In addition,
understanding customer needs helps businesses stay competitive.
So, quality, good service, and effective management are key factors in business success.

擅长学习和说语言的人

 Describe a person who is good at learning and speaking new languages
 You should say:
 How you got to know him or her
 How he or she learns a new language
 What languages he or she can speak
 And explain how you feel about him/her

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“人物类 + 能力类”话题，要求你描述一个擅长学习和说外语的人。需要说清：你是怎么认识他或她的、他
或她如何学习新语言、会说哪些语言，以及你对他或她的感受。整体以现在时为主。重点在于学习方法、语言能力
以及你对这个人的评价。
思路方面
这里可以选择朋友、同学或同事。思路上可以先说清你们是怎么认识的；然后要说清他或她学习语言的方法，比如
日常练习、模仿或交流；接着可以重点说他或她会哪些语言；最后表达你对这个人的感受，比如佩服、受到鼓励或
觉得很厉害。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个人是谁以及你如何认识他或她；
第二段：描述他或她学习语言的方法和会说的语言；
第三段：解释你对他或她的感受，并总结原因。

44
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The person I want to talk about is my colleague. 我想说的人是我的同事。
描述感受  I got to know him at work. 我是在工作中认识他的。
      He has a strong interest in languages. 他对语言很感兴趣。
      He enjoys communicating with different people. 他喜欢和不同的人交流。
      Learning languages is part of his daily life. 学语言已经成了他日常生活的一部分。
      He practices listening and speaking every day. 他每天练习听力和口语。
      He likes reading short articles in foreign languages. 他喜欢阅读外语短文章。
      He often watches videos without subtitles. 他经常不看字幕看视频。
      He can speak English, Spanish, and some Korean. 他会说英语、西班牙语和一些韩语。
      He learns by using the language actively. 他通过主动使用语言来学习。
      I think he is very disciplined. 我觉得他非常自律。
      His progress is really impressive. 他的进步非常惊人。
      He motivates me to study harder. 他激励我更努力学习。
      I admire his confidence and patience. 我佩服他的自信和耐心。
      That is why I respect him a lot. 这就是我很尊敬他的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A person who is very good at learning and speaking new languages is one of my colleagues. I got to know him a
few years ago when we started working in the same office. From our first conversation, I noticed that he was
very interested in different cultures and languages.
He has a very consistent way of learning new languages. Every day, he spends some time practicing listening
and speaking. He often watches foreign videos without subtitles and reads short articles in different languages
during breaks. Instead of only memorizing vocabulary, he prefers using the language actively in real
conversations. He also talks with online language partners and sometimes records his own voice to improve
pronunciation. According to him, regular practice is more important than studying for many hours once in a
while.
At the moment, he can speak English fluently, and he also speaks Spanish quite well. Besides that, he knows
some Korean because he has been learning it for the past two years. What surprised me most is that he can
switch between languages very naturally during conversations.
I really admire him because learning languages requires patience and discipline, and he has both qualities. His
attitude also makes language learning seem less stressful and more enjoyable. After seeing his progress, I
became more motivated to improve my own English speaking skills. That is why I respect him a lot and think he
is truly talented in this area.

Part 3
Question 1: Are there many people who can speak foreign languages in your country?

 解题思路：现象类问题。可以从“英语教育普及”和“实际使用能力差异”展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, many people in my country study foreign languages, especially English. Students usually begin learning
English at school from a young age.
However, there is still a difference between learning a language and speaking it fluently. People living in large
cities or working in international companies often use foreign languages more confidently. Older people may
have fewer chances to practice.
So, many people can speak some foreign languages, but fluency levels are quite different.

Question 2: Does speaking other languages help at work?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“工作机会”“沟通能力”“国际合作”展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, speaking other languages can help a lot at work. It gives people more job opportunities, especially in
international companies or tourism-related industries.

45
Knowing another language also improves communication with clients and coworkers from different countries.
It can help people build stronger professional relationships.
So, language skills are valuable because they improve both career opportunities and workplace communication
Question 3: Do people learn any languages other than English?

 解题思路：现象类问题。可以从“教育需求”“兴趣”“工作机会”展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, some people learn languages other than English. Popular choices include Japanese, Korean, French, and
Spanish.
Some people learn these languages because of work or study opportunities, while others are interested in
foreign culture, music, or travel. Young people are especially interested in learning languages connected to
entertainment and social media.
So, although English is the most common foreign language, many people also study other languages for
different reasons.
Question 4: Why is it easier for children to learn new things than for adults?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“大脑发展”“时间精力”“害怕犯错较少”展开。
Sample Answer:
It is often easier for children to learn new things because their brains are still developing and adapting quickly.
Children are naturally curious and willing to try new experiences.
They are also less afraid of making mistakes, which helps them learn more confidently. Adults, on the other
hand, are usually busier and may feel more pressure or stress when learning.
So, flexibility, curiosity, and fewer worries make learning easier for children.

Event【事】

在团队中工作

 Describe a time when you worked in a group
 You should say:
 What you did
 Who you worked with
 What problems you faced
 And explain why you worked in the group

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“经历类 + 合作类”话题，要求你描述一次团队合作经历。需要说清：你们做了什么、和谁一起合作、过程
中遇到了什么问题，以及为什么需要团队合作。整体以过去时为主。重点在于合作过程和问题解决。
思路方面
这里可以选择学校项目、比赛或活动准备。思路上可以先说清任务内容和团队成员；然后要说清你们合作过程中遇
到的问题，比如时间不够、意见不同；接着可以重点说大家如何解决问题；最后说明为什么这项任务需要团队合
作。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这次团队合作是什么，以及你和谁一起完成；
第二段：描述你们遇到的问题和合作过程；
第三段：解释为什么需要团队合作，并总结你的感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The experience happened during high school. 这次经历发生在高中。
描述感受  I worked with several classmates. 我和几个同学一起合作。
      We were preparing a group presentation. 我们当时在准备小组展示。
      Everyone had different responsibilities. 每个人都有不同任务。
      The project was important for our final grade. 这个项目对期末成绩很重要。
      We faced some communication problems. 我们遇到了一些沟通问题。
      Some members had different ideas. 有些成员有不同想法。
      We also had limited time. 我们时间也很紧。
      In the end, we divided the work clearly. 最后我们明确分工。
      We helped each other finish the project. 我们互相帮助完成了项目。
      I learned the importance of teamwork. 我学到了团队合作的重要性。
      Working together made the task easier. 一起合作让任务变得更容易。
      I felt proud of our final result. 我为最后的成果感到自豪。
      The experience improved my communication skills. 这次经历提升了我的沟通能力。
      That is why I still remember this experience clearly. 这就是我至今仍记得这次经历的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A time when I worked in a group was during high school when my classmates and I prepared a group
presentation for an English class. The project was very important because it counted toward our final grade, so
everyone took it seriously. There were five students in the group, including me, and each person had different
responsibilities.

47
Our topic was about environmental protection, and we needed to collect information, make slides, and give a
presentation in front of the class. At the beginning, we faced some problems because everyone had different
ideas about how to organize the project. In addition, we did not have much free time because we also had
other homework and exams. Sometimes communication became difficult, and a few members worried that we
would not finish everything on time.
In the end, we solved the problem by dividing the work more clearly. One person focused on finding
information, another worked on the design of the slides, and others practiced speaking. We also created a
simple schedule so that everyone knew what to do. Because we worked together and helped each other, we
finished the project successfully and received positive feedback from the teacher.
I think we worked in a group because the task was too large for one person to complete alone. This experience
taught me the importance of teamwork and communication. It also helped me become more patient and
organized when working with others.

Part 3
Question 1: Why do some people prefer to work by themselves?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“自由度”“工作效率”“减少社交压力”三个角度展开。
Sample Answer:
Some people prefer to work by themselves because they enjoy freedom and independence. When working
alone, they can decide their own schedule and working style.
Working alone may also improve concentration. Some people feel more efficient without too many meetings or
discussions. In addition, introverted people may feel more comfortable without strong social pressure.
So, people who value independence and quiet working environments often prefer to work alone.

Question 2: What should a leader do to make team members want to follow him or her?
 解题思路：解决方式类问题。可以从“沟通”“公平”“以身作则”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
A good leader should communicate clearly and respect team members. When people understand goals and
feel listened to, they are more willing to follow the leader.
Fairness is also important. Leaders should treat everyone equally and recognize hard work. In addition, leaders
should set a good example by working responsibly and staying positive.
So, strong communication, fairness, and good behavior help leaders gain trust and support from their teams.

Question 3: Should students learn to do group work?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“团队合作”“沟通能力”“未来工作需求”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, students should learn to do group work. Group activities help students improve teamwork and
communication skills. They learn how to share ideas and solve problems together.
Group work is also useful for future jobs because many workplaces require cooperation. In addition, students
can learn from classmates with different opinions and strengths.
So, group work not only improves learning but also helps students develop important social skills.

Question 4: What group tasks are there in schools?
 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“课堂项目”“体育活动”“讨论展示”三个方面举例。

Sample Answer:
There are many group tasks in schools. Students often work together on projects, presentations, or research
activities. These tasks require students to share ideas and divide responsibilities.
Sports activities are also common group tasks because students need teamwork and cooperation. In some
classes, students join discussions or solve problems together in groups.
So, group tasks help students improve communication, teamwork, and problem-solving skills.

早起经历

 Describe a time when you got up early
 You should say:
 When it was
 What you did
 Why you got up early
 And how you felt about it

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“经历类 + 日常生活类”话题，要求你描述一次你早起的经历。需要说清：是什么时候、你做了什么、为什
么要早起，以及你对这次经历的感受。整体以过去时为主。重点在于早起原因和当时的体验。
思路方面
这里可以选择考试、旅行、活动或重要安排。思路上可以先说清时间背景；然后要说清你早起后做了什么；接着可
以重点说为什么必须早起；最后描述你当时的感受，比如疲惫、兴奋或满足。
结构方面
第一段：介绍什么时候早起以及原因；
第二段：描述你早起后做了什么；
第三段：解释你的感受，并总结这次经历。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The experience happened last summer. 这次经历发生在去年夏天。
描述感受  I had to wake up very early. 我必须很早起床。
      It was earlier than my normal routine. 这比我平时起得早。
      I usually do not wake up that early. 我平时不那么早起。
      The day was quite important to me. 那天对我来说很重要。
      I got up at around five o’clock. 我大约五点起床。
      I quickly prepared everything. 我很快准备好了一切。
      I went out before sunrise. 我在日出前出了门。
      I spent most of the morning traveling. 我大半个早上都在路上。
      The streets were quiet and peaceful. 街道很安静。
      At first, I felt very sleepy. 一开始我很困。
      Later, I started to feel excited. 后来我开始感到兴奋。
      The fresh morning air made me feel relaxed. 清晨的新鲜空气让我很放松。
      I felt satisfied after finishing everything. 完成事情后我很满足。
      That is why I still remember this experience clearly. 这就是我至今还清楚记得这次经历的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A time when I got up early was last summer when I went on a short trip with my friends. We planned to take
an early train, so I had to wake up at around five o’clock in the morning, which was much earlier than my
normal routine. Usually, I prefer sleeping a little longer, so getting up that early was not easy for me.
After waking up, I quickly washed my face, packed my bag, and checked my tickets and phone one more time
before leaving home. I went out before sunrise and met my friends at the train station. During the journey, we
talked about our travel plans and watched the city slowly become brighter as the sun came up. The streets
were much quieter than usual, and the fresh morning air made everything feel calm and peaceful.
I got up early that day because I did not want to miss the train, and I was also excited about the trip. At first, I
felt very sleepy and tired, but later I became more energetic and happy. Although waking up early was difficult,
I felt satisfied because the whole day went smoothly and we had a wonderful trip together.

49
This experience also made me realize that getting up early can sometimes make a day feel longer and more
productive. That is why I still remember this experience clearly.

Part 3
Question 1: Is it good to arrive early in any situation?

 解题思路：个人举例类问题。可以简单描述人物和原因。
Sample Answer:
Yes, I know several people who like to get up early, especially older family members. They believe mornings are
quiet and peaceful, which helps them start the day in a good mood.
Some people exercise, read, or prepare work early in the morning because they feel more energetic at that
time. My friend also likes getting up early to avoid traffic and have a more organized day.
So, people who enjoy quiet time and good routines often prefer waking up early.

Question 2: Do you know anyone who likes to get up early?
 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“工作学习”“生活习惯”“效率感”展开。

Sample Answer:
People get up early for different reasons. Many people need to start work or school early, especially in busy
cities with heavy traffic.
Some people also believe mornings are the most peaceful and productive time of the day. They use the early
hours to exercise, read, or prepare for work. Older people often wake up early because of long-term habits.
So, work needs, personal routines, and lifestyle habits are the main reasons people get up early.

Question 3: Why do people get up early?
 解题思路：情境类问题。可以从“正式场合”“交通不确定性”“礼貌”展开。

Sample Answer:
People usually need to arrive early for important or formal occasions. Job interviews, exams, and business
meetings are good examples because being late may cause problems or leave a bad impression.
People also arrive early for flights, concerts, or weddings to avoid traffic and prepare calmly. Arriving early
shows respect and good planning.
So, events with strict schedules or social importance usually require people to come early.

Question 4: What kinds of occasions need people to arrive early?
 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“工作学习”“个人习惯”“夜晚安静”展开。

Sample Answer:
Some people like to stay up late because they feel more relaxed and focused at night. The environment is
usually quieter, so they can study, work, or enjoy hobbies without interruption.
Others stay up late because of busy schedules or entertainment, such as watching videos or playing games.
Some people naturally prefer sleeping later due to personal habits.
So, lifestyle, work pressure, and personal preference are common reasons for staying up late.

50
喜欢的现场体育赛事

 Describe a live sports event you watched and liked
 You should say:
 What it was
 When and where you watched it
 Who you watched it with
 And explain why you liked it

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“事件经历类 + 体育类”话题，要求你描述一次你看过并且喜欢的现场体育赛事。需要说清：这是什么比
赛、你什么时候和在哪里看的、和谁一起看的，以及你为什么喜欢这场比赛。整体以过去时为主。重点在于现场氛
围和个人感受。
思路方面
这里可以选择足球、篮球或其他热门体育赛事。思路上可以先说清比赛类型；然后要说清观看时间、地点和同行的
人；接着可以重点说比赛过程中让你印象深刻的地方；最后说明你为什么喜欢这场比赛。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这场体育赛事是什么，以及你什么时候、在哪里观看；
第二段：描述比赛过程和你和谁一起看；
第三段：解释为什么你喜欢这场比赛，并总结感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The sports event I want to talk about was a basketball game. 我想说的体育赛事是一场篮球比赛。
描述感受  I watched it last year. 我是去年看的。
      The match took place in a large stadium. 比赛在一个大型体育馆举行。
      Many fans attended the event. 很多球迷都去了现场。
      The atmosphere was very exciting. 现场气氛非常热烈。
      I watched the game with my friends. 我和朋友一起看了比赛。
      The players performed very well. 球员们表现得很好。
      The score stayed very close during the game. 比分一直很接近。
      Everyone cheered loudly during important moments. 关键时刻大家都大声欢呼。
      The final part of the game was especially exciting. 比赛最后阶段尤其精彩。
      I enjoyed the energy of the crowd. 我很喜欢现场观众的氛围。
      Watching it live felt very different from TV. 现场观看和电视上很不一样。
      The game kept me excited the whole time. 整场比赛都让我很兴奋。
      It became a memorable experience for me. 它成了我难忘的经历。
      That is why I liked this sports event so much. 这就是我这么喜欢这场赛事的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A live sports event I watched and really liked was a basketball game between two professional teams. I
watched it last year in a large indoor stadium in my city. It was my first time watching a professional basketball
game live, so I felt quite excited even before the match started.
I went there with two of my close friends because we all enjoy watching basketball. When we arrived at the
stadium, there were already many fans wearing team shirts and cheering loudly. The atmosphere was much
more energetic than I expected. During the game, both teams played very well, and the score stayed close for
most of the match. The most exciting part was near the end, when one team scored an important three-point
shot just before the final whistle. Everyone around us stood up and shouted at the same time.

51
I liked this sports event mainly because of the atmosphere and the excitement of watching everything happen
in real time. Watching the game live felt completely different from watching it on television. I could hear the
crowd, see the players clearly, and feel the energy inside the stadium. In addition, spending time with my
friends made the experience even more enjoyable. It became one of my favorite sports memories, and that is
why I still remember this event so clearly.

Question 1: Why do some people like to watch sports events?

So, sports events are popular because they provide entertainment, emotional excitement, and opportunities
for social interaction.

Question 2: Where do people normally watch sports events?

on TV or online with family and friends.
Some sports fans prefer watching events in stadiums because the atmosphere is more exciting and energetic.
In addition, people sometimes watch important games in restaurants or public places together.
So, people choose different places depending on convenience, cost, and the kind of experience they want.

Question 3: What are the advantages of watching sports events online?

games anywhere with a phone or computer.
Online platforms also offer more choices, such as highlights, replays, and different camera angles. Some people

Question 4: What sports matches are suitable for children to attend?

or swimming competitions are popular because children can enjoy the atmosphere and learn teamwork.

and audiences. Matches that are too violent or aggressive may not be suitable for younger children.
So, sports events that are fun, educational, and safe are the best choices for children.

52
坏掉的电子设备

 Describe a time when you had a problem with using an electronic device
 You should say:
 When it happened
 Where it happened
 What the problem was
 And explain how you solved the problem at last

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“事件经历类 + 科技类”话题，要求你描述一次使用电子设备时遇到的问题。需要说清：事情发生在什么时
候、在哪里、出现了什么问题，以及最后你是如何解决的。整体以过去时为主。重点在于问题过程和解决方式。
思路方面
这里可以选择手机、电脑、平板或耳机等设备。思路上可以先说清事情发生的时间和地点；然后要说清具体问题是
什么；接着可以重点说你当时的反应和解决过程；最后表达你从这次经历中的感受。
结构方面
第一段：介绍什么时候、在哪里发生了这个问题；
第二段：描述电子设备出现的问题以及你的反应；
第三段：解释你最后如何解决问题，并总结感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The problem happened a few months ago. 这个问题发生在几个月前。
描述感受  I was using my laptop at home. 我当时正在家里用笔记本电脑。
      The device suddenly stopped working properly. 设备突然无法正常工作。
      I depended on it a lot at that time. 当时我很依赖这个设备。
      The situation made me feel nervous. 当时让我有点紧张。
      The screen suddenly went black. 屏幕突然黑了。
      The device could not restart normally. 设备无法正常重启。
      I tried several simple methods first. 我先尝试了几个简单方法。
      I searched for solutions online. 我在网上搜索了解决办法。
      In the end, I asked a technician for help. 最后我找了技术人员帮忙。
      At first, I felt stressed and worried. 一开始我感到紧张和担心。
      I was afraid of losing important files. 我担心重要文件丢失。
      Luckily, the problem was solved in the end. 幸运的是最后问题解决了。
      The experience taught me to back up my files. 这次经历让我学会备份文件。
      That is why I still remember this experience clearly. 这就是我现在还记得这次经历的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A time when I had a problem with using an electronic device was a few months ago when I was using my
laptop at home. I depended on it heavily because I needed it for both work and study, so the problem made
me feel quite nervous.
One evening, while I was working on an important document, the screen suddenly went black. At first, I
thought the battery had run out, but after charging it for some time, the laptop still could not restart normally. I
tried several simple methods by myself, such as restarting the computer and checking the power connection,
but nothing worked. At that moment, I became worried because I was afraid of losing some important files
stored on the laptop.

53
After trying to solve the problem alone, I searched online for possible solutions. I followed some instructions
from a technology forum, but the device still did not work properly. In the end, I decided to take the laptop to a
repair shop near my home. The technician checked the computer and told me there was a problem with the
system software. Luckily, he managed to repair it without deleting my files.
I felt very relieved after the laptop started working again. This experience taught me that electronic devices can
fail unexpectedly, so it is important to back up important files regularly. That is why I still remember this
experience very clearly.

Part 3
Question 1: Why are people keen on buying new electronic devices?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“功能升级”“社会趋势”“便利性”三个方面展开。
Sample Answer:
People are keen on buying new electronic devices because technology changes quickly. New products often
have better functions, faster speed, and more convenient features.
Some people also buy new devices because of social trends. They want to keep up with modern lifestyles or
own fashionable products. In addition, electronic devices help people with work, study, and entertainment.
So, improved technology, convenience, and social influence make electronic products very attractive to many
people.

Question 2: What impact do electronic devices have on people?
 解题思路：影响类问题。可以从“便利性”和“负面依赖”两方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Electronic devices have changed people’s lives a lot. They make communication, learning, and work much more
convenient. People can quickly get information and stay connected through phones or computers.
However, electronic devices can also create problems. Some people spend too much time on screens and
become less active. Overuse may also affect sleep and face-to-face communication.
So, electronic devices bring many benefits, but people should use them in a balanced way.

遇到的科技问题

 Describe a challenging technological problem you faced
 You should say:
 What the problem was
 When and where you faced it
 How challenging it was
 And explain how you solved it

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“事件经历类 + 科技类”话题，要求你描述一次你遇到的技术问题。需要说清：问题是什么、你什么时候和
在哪里遇到的、它有多困难，以及你是如何解决它的。整体以过去时为主。重点在于问题解决过程和你的感受。
思路方面
这里可以选择电脑、手机、网络或软件相关问题。思路上可以先说清发生的时间和场景；然后要说清具体技术问
题；接着可以重点说这个问题为什么困难；最后说明你是如何一步步解决它的。
结构方面
第一段：介绍你遇到的技术问题是什么；
第二段：描述问题发生的时间、地点以及困难之处；
第三段：解释你是如何解决问题的，并表达你的感受。

54
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The problem happened while I was using my laptop. 问题发生在我用笔记本电脑的时候。
描述感受  It happened a few months ago. 这件事发生在几个月前。
      I was working on an important task. 我当时正在处理一个重要任务。
      The situation made me feel stressed. 当时让我很紧张。
      I had never faced this problem before. 我以前从没遇到过这个问题。
      My computer suddenly stopped working properly. 我的电脑突然无法正常工作。
      The screen froze several times. 屏幕卡住了好几次。
      I could not save my files. 我无法保存文件。
      The problem was difficult because I had limited time. 这个问题很困难，因为我时间不够。
      I tried several methods to fix it. 我尝试了很多方法修复。
      At first, I felt nervous and frustrated. 一开始我既紧张又沮丧。
      Later, I became more calm and focused. 后来我变得更冷静、更专注。
      I learned some useful technical skills. 我学到了一些有用的技术技巧。
      The experience improved my problem-solving ability. 这次经历提升了我的解决问题能力。
      That is why I still remember this experience clearly. 这就是我至今还记得这次经历的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A challenging technological problem I faced happened a few months ago while I was using my laptop at home.
At that time, I was preparing an important presentation, and I had already spent several hours working on it.
Suddenly, my computer stopped responding properly, and the screen froze several times.
The situation became very stressful because I could not save my files or continue my work. I tried restarting the
laptop, but the same problem appeared again after a few minutes. Since the presentation deadline was close, I
felt quite nervous and frustrated. What made the problem more difficult was that I had never experienced this
kind of issue before, so I did not know the exact cause of it.
After calming down, I started searching for possible solutions on my phone. I watched a few online videos and
read some simple technical guides. Eventually, I realized that the laptop memory was almost full and several
unnecessary programs were running in the background. I deleted some large files, closed unused applications,
and restarted the system again. After that, the laptop worked much more smoothly, and I was finally able to
finish my presentation on time.
Although the experience was stressful, I learned some useful technical skills and became more confident when
dealing with technology problems. That is why I still remember this experience very clearly.

Part 3
Question 1: What are the advantages and disadvantages of AI?

 解题思路：利弊类问题。可以从“效率提升”和“依赖与失业风险”两个方面展开，体现平衡观点。
Sample Answer:
AI has many advantages. It helps people work more efficiently and saves time in areas such as healthcare,
education, and transportation. AI can also handle repetitive tasks and provide quick information.
However, AI also has disadvantages. Some people worry that AI may replace human jobs, especially simple or
repetitive work. In addition, people may become too dependent on technology and lose some practical skills.
So, AI is very useful, but it should be used carefully and responsibly.

Question 2: Do you think people today should learn about AI technology?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“社会发展”“工作需求”“正确使用”展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, I think people today should learn about AI technology because it is becoming part of daily life and work.
Understanding AI helps people use technology more effectively and responsibly.
AI is already used in education, healthcare, and business, so basic knowledge can improve future job
opportunities. It also helps people understand the risks and limits of AI.
So, learning about AI is important for adapting to modern society and future development.

55
Question 3: Should children learn to use AI?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以采取“应该，但需要指导”的平衡观点。
Sample Answer:
Yes, I think children should learn to use AI because it will become more important in the future. AI can help
children study, search for information, and improve learning efficiency.
However, children also need guidance from teachers and parents. They should learn how to use AI responsibly
and not depend on it too much. Critical thinking is still very important.
So, learning AI is useful for children, but proper guidance is necessary.

Question 4: How can AI help in our lives?

 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“学习”“工作”“日常生活便利”展开。
Sample Answer:
AI can help people in many areas of life. In education, AI can provide quick information and personalized
learning support. At work, AI improves efficiency by handling repetitive tasks and organizing data.
AI is also useful in daily life. For example, smart devices help people manage schedules, find directions, or
control home equipment more easily.
So, AI makes life more convenient, efficient, and organized for many people.

重要决定

 Describe an important decision that you made
 You should say:
 What the decision was
 How you made your decision
 What the results of the decision were
 And explain why it was important

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“经历类 + 决策类”话题，要求你描述一个你曾经做出的重要决定。需要说清：这个决定是什么、你是如何
做出决定的、结果如何，以及为什么这个决定很重要。整体以过去时为主，最后可用现在时总结影响。重点在于思
考过程和决定带来的变化。
思路方面
这里可以选择学习、工作或生活中的重要决定。思路上可以先说清决定内容；然后要说清你是如何思考和做出决定
的；接着可以重点说这个决定带来的结果；最后说明为什么它对你来说很重要。
结构方面
第一段：介绍你做出的重要决定是什么；
第二段：描述你如何做出这个决定以及结果如何；
第三段：解释为什么这个决定很重要，并表达你的感受。

 背景描述                                           答题参考
       The decision I want to talk about was related to my studies. 我想说的决定和学习有关。
 具体描述  I made this decision a few years ago. 我是几年前做出这个决定的。
56     At that time, I felt uncertain about the future. 当时我对未来有些迷茫。
       I spent a long time thinking about it. 我花了很长时间思考。
       It was not an easy decision to make. 这不是一个容易做出的决定。
       I discussed the situation with my family and friends. 我和家人朋友讨论了这件事。
       I compared different choices carefully. 我认真比较了不同选择。
       In the end, I followed my own interests. 最后我选择了自己的兴趣。
描述感受  The decision changed my daily life a lot. 这个决定很大程度改变了我的生活。
      The result was better than I expected. 结果比我想象中更好。
      The experience helped me become more independent. 这次经历让我更独立。
      I learned how to make careful decisions. 我学会了如何认真做决定。
      I feel proud of the choice I made. 我为自己的选择感到自豪。
      The decision influenced my future plans. 这个决定影响了我的未来规划。
      That is why I think it was very important. 这就是我认为它非常重要的原因。

Sample Answer ：
An important decision that I made was choosing my university major a few years ago. At that time, I was
finishing high school and thinking seriously about my future. I felt quite uncertain because there were many
different options, and I knew this decision could influence my career and daily life in the future.
To make the decision, I spent a long time thinking carefully about my interests and strengths. I also talked with
my family, teachers, and friends to hear different opinions. Some people suggested choosing a major that was
more stable or popular, while others encouraged me to follow my personal interests. After comparing several
choices, I finally decided to choose a major that matched my interests and communication skills instead of
simply following other people’s expectations.
The result of this decision was much better than I expected. During university, I became more motivated to
study because I genuinely enjoyed the subjects I was learning. I also met many people with similar interests,
which made my university life more enjoyable and meaningful. In addition, this decision helped me become
more confident and independent, because it was one of the first major choices I made by myself.
I think this decision was very important because it influenced both my education and my future career plans. It
taught me the importance of understanding myself before making big decisions, and I still feel proud of the
choice I made.
