别人帮助解决问题

 Describe a time when a person did something to help you solve a problem
 You should say:
 Who the person is
 What the problem was
 How he/she helped you
 And explain how you felt about the experience

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“经历类 + 人物帮助类”话题，要求你描述一次别人帮助你解决问题的经历。需要说清：这个人是谁、你遇
到了什么问题、他或她是如何帮助你的，以及你对这次经历的感受。整体以过去时为主。重点在于问题解决过程和
你的情绪变化。
思路方面
这里可以选择学习、工作或生活中的真实问题。思路上可以先说清问题背景；然后要说清这个人是如何帮助你的；
接着可以重点说问题最后是如何解决的；最后表达你对这个人的感谢和感受。
结构方面
第一段：介绍帮助你的人是谁以及你遇到了什么问题；
第二段：描述他或她是如何帮助你的；
第三段：解释你对这次经历的感受，并总结原因。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The person I want to talk about is my friend. 我想说的人是我的朋友。
描述感受  The experience happened last year. 这件事发生在去年。
      At that time, I was under a lot of pressure. 当时我压力很大。
      I faced a difficult problem at work. 我在工作中遇到了困难。
      I did not know how to solve it by myself. 我当时不知道该怎么自己解决。
      He listened to my problem patiently. 他耐心听我说问题。
      He gave me some practical advice. 他给了我一些实际建议。
      He helped me organize my work step by step. 他帮我一步一步整理工作。
      Together, we found a solution. 我们一起找到了办法。
      The problem became much easier after his help. 在他的帮助下问题容易多了。
      I felt very grateful to him. 我非常感谢他。
      His support made me feel less stressed. 他的帮助让我没那么焦虑。
      I learned the importance of asking for help. 我学会了寻求帮助的重要性。
      The experience improved our friendship. 这次经历也让我们的友谊更深。
      That is why I still remember this experience clearly. 这就是我至今还记得这次经历的原因。

58
Sample Answer ：
A time when a person helped me solve a problem was last year when I was preparing an important
presentation for work. At that time, I had a lot of tasks to finish, and I felt extremely stressed because the
deadline was very close. The person who helped me was one of my close friends, who is also very organized
and good at solving practical problems.
The main problem was that my presentation materials were incomplete, and I could not organize my ideas
clearly. I spent hours trying to improve the slides, but everything still looked messy. When my friend noticed
that I was worried, he offered to help me. First, he listened carefully while I explained the situation. Then he
suggested dividing the presentation into smaller sections and focusing on the most important points instead of
trying to include too much information. He also helped me improve the structure of the slides and gave me
advice on how to speak more confidently.
After following his suggestions, the presentation became much clearer and easier to understand. In the end,
everything went smoothly, and I received positive feedback from my manager. I felt very grateful because his
help reduced a lot of my stress and gave me more confidence.
This experience taught me that asking for help is sometimes very important. It also made me appreciate my
friend even more because he stayed patient and supportive throughout the whole process.

花很久时间回复信息

 Describe a time when you received a message or email and it took you a long time to reply
 You should say:
 Who this person was
 What the message/email was about
 Why you spent a lot of time replying
 And explain how you felt about the experience

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“经历类 + 沟通类”话题，要求你描述一次你收到消息或邮件后花了很长时间回复的经历。需要说清：发消
息的人是谁、消息内容是什么、为什么你花了很久回复，以及你对这次经历的感受。整体以过去时为主。重点在于
思考过程和情绪变化。
思路方面
这里可以选择老师、朋友或工作相关的人发来的消息。思路上可以先说清发消息的人和消息内容；然后要说清为什
么回复困难，比如内容重要、需要认真考虑；接着可以重点说你是如何准备回复的；最后表达你的感受。
结构方面
第一段：介绍是谁给你发了消息以及内容是什么；
第二段：描述为什么你花了很长时间回复；
第三段：解释你的感受，并总结这次经历。

59
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The message was sent by my manager. 这条消息是我经理发来的。
描述感受  I received it a few months ago. 我是几个月前收到的。
      The message was quite important. 这条消息非常重要。
      I noticed it late in the evening. 我是在晚上看到它的。
      At first, I did not know how to reply. 一开始我不知道怎么回复。
      The message was related to work plans. 这条消息和工作安排有关。
      I needed time to organize my thoughts. 我需要时间整理思路。
      I rewrote my reply several times. 我改了好几次回复。
      I wanted my answer to sound professional. 我希望回复听起来专业一些。
      In the end, I finally sent the message. 最后我终于把消息发出去了。
      I felt nervous at the beginning. 一开始我很紧张。
      Later, I became more confident. 后来我变得更有信心。
      The experience taught me to communicate carefully. 这次经历让我学会认真沟通。
      I felt relieved after sending the reply. 发出回复后我松了一口气。
      That is why I still remember this experience clearly. 这就是我至今还记得这次经历的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A time when I received a message and spent a long time replying was a few months ago when my manager
sent me an important work-related message. I saw it late in the evening after finishing my daily tasks, and at
first I felt quite nervous because the message was about a new project arrangement.
In the message, my manager asked whether I would be willing to take on more responsibilities in an upcoming
project. It sounded like a good opportunity, but at the same time, I was worried because I already had a busy
schedule. I did not want to reply too quickly without thinking carefully. Because of that, I spent a long time
considering my answer and organizing my thoughts. I even rewrote the message several times because I
wanted my reply to sound professional, polite, and clear.
In the end, I replied the next morning after thinking about the advantages and disadvantages carefully. I
accepted the opportunity but also honestly explained my concerns about workload and time management.
Fortunately, my manager understood my situation and appreciated my careful response.
At first, the whole experience made me feel stressed and uncertain, but later I felt more confident and relieved
after sending the reply. This experience taught me that important messages sometimes require time and
careful communication instead of quick reactions. That is why I still remember this experience very clearly.

Part 3
Question 1: When do people spend a long time responding to others’ messages?

 解题思路：情境类问题。可以从“内容重要”“情绪考虑”“工作正式场合”展开。
Sample Answer:
People usually spend a long time replying to messages when the topic is important or sensitive. For example,
work emails or serious discussions often require careful thinking before answering.
Some people also take longer when they want to avoid misunderstanding or express feelings clearly. In
personal relationships, people may think carefully before replying to emotional messages.
So, people often spend more time replying when the message is important or requires careful communication.

Question 2: In what situations do people not respond to messages right away?

 解题思路：情境类问题。可以从“工作学习忙”“情绪考虑”“正式回复需要时间”展开。
Sample Answer:
People may not reply to messages immediately when they are busy with work, study, or meetings. In these
situations, they may not have time to answer carefully.
Some people also delay replies when messages are emotional or important because they want to think before
responding. In addition, people sometimes ignore messages when they want personal space or rest.
So, delays in replying are often related to time, emotions, or the need for careful communication.

60
Question 3: What would you do if you haven’t received a reply after sending out a message?

 解题思路：情境类问题。可以从“耐心等待”“再次确认”“理解对方忙碌”展开。

Sample Answer:
If I do not receive a reply after sending a message, I usually wait for some time first because the other person
may be busy.
If the message is important, I may send a polite reminder later or try another way to contact them. However, I
try not to become impatient because people sometimes need time to respond.
So, I prefer to stay polite and understanding when waiting for replies.

花费甚少的外出日

 Describe a special day out that cost you little money/didn’t cost you much
 You should say:
 When the day was
 Where you went
 How much you spent
 And explain how you feel about the day

教师的解析：
这是一道“经历类 + 休闲活动类”话题，要求你描述一次花费不多但很特别的外出经历。需要说清：那是什么时候、
你去了哪里、花了多少钱，以及你对那一天的感受。整体以过去时为主。重点在于低花费但高满足感。
思路方面
这里可以选择公园、海边、城市散步或短途出行。思路上可以先说清时间和地点；然后要说清当天做了什么以及实
际花费；接着可以重点说为什么虽然便宜但依然特别；最后表达你的感受。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这一天是什么时候以及你去了哪里；
第二段：描述当天的活动和花费；
第三段：解释为什么这一天特别，并表达你的感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The special day happened last spring. 这个特别的日子发生在去年春天。
描述感受  I went out with one of my close friends. 我和一个好朋友一起出去。
      We decided to have a simple day trip. 我们决定来一次简单的一日游。
      The weather was warm and comfortable. 天气温暖又舒服。
      We did not plan to spend much money. 我们原本就没打算花很多钱。
      We visited a public park near the city center. 我们去了市中心附近的一个公园。
      Most activities there were free. 那里的大部分活动都是免费的。
      We bought some snacks and drinks. 我们买了一些零食和饮料。
      In total, I spent very little money. 总共我花的钱很少。
      We spent hours walking and talking. 我们花了很长时间散步聊天。
      The day felt relaxing and meaningful. 那一天既放松又有意义。
      I enjoyed the simple atmosphere. 我喜欢那种简单轻松的氛围。
      It reminded me that happiness does not always require money. 它让我意识到快乐不一定需要花很多
      钱。
      I made some wonderful memories that day. 那天我留下了很美好的回忆。
      That is why I still remember the day clearly. 这就是我至今还记得那一天的原因。

61
Sample Answer ：
A special day out that did not cost me much happened last spring when I went to a public park with one of my
close friends. At that time, both of us were quite busy with work and study, so we wanted to relax for a day
without spending too much money. The weather was warm and sunny, which made the whole trip even more
enjoyable.
We visited a large park near the city center. Most activities there were completely free, so we mainly spent
time walking around, talking, and enjoying the natural scenery. We also sat near a lake for a while and watched
people riding bicycles and flying kites. The only things we bought were a few snacks and drinks from a small
store nearby. In total, I probably spent less than fifty yuan that day, which was much cheaper than going
shopping or eating in expensive restaurants.
Even though the trip was simple and inexpensive, I still think it was a very special day. I felt relaxed because
there was no pressure, no busy schedule, and no need to spend a lot of money. Sometimes expensive activities
can actually feel tiring, but that day felt natural and comfortable. Spending time with a good friend and
enjoying a peaceful environment made me very happy.
This experience reminded me that meaningful moments do not always need to cost a lot of money. That is why
I still remember this special day very clearly.

Part 3
Question 1: Do people like to spend their leisure time out in your country?

 解题思路：现象类问题。可以从“放松需求”“社交活动”“年轻人与家庭差异”展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, many people in my country like spending their leisure time outside. They often go shopping, eat at
restaurants, or visit parks with friends and family.
Young people may enjoy cafés, cinemas, or sports activities, while families often choose parks or tourist places
during weekends. However, some people still prefer staying at home to rest or watch videos.
So, outdoor leisure activities are popular because they help people relax and socialize.

Question 2: How do people spend their leisure time in your country?

 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“线上娱乐”“社交活动”“户外休闲”展开。
Sample Answer:
People in my country spend their leisure time in many ways. Some enjoy watching videos, playing games, or
using social media at home.
Others prefer going out with friends or family. They may go shopping, eat at restaurants, or visit parks and
tourist places. Exercise and sports are also becoming more popular.
So, people choose different leisure activities depending on their interests and lifestyle.

Question 3: How does technology affect the way people spend their leisure time?
 解题思路：影响类问题。可以从“线上娱乐增加”和“户外活动减少”两个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Technology has greatly changed the way people spend their leisure time. Many people now relax by watching
videos, playing games, or using social media on their phones.
Technology also makes entertainment more convenient because people can access music, movies, and online
activities anytime. However, some people spend less time doing outdoor activities or meeting friends face to
face.
So, technology makes leisure activities more convenient, but it may also reduce real-world interaction.

Question 4: Do you think only old people have time for leisure?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以反驳绝对化观点，说明不同年龄都有休闲需求。

 英文回答：
Sample Answer:
No, I don’t think only old people have time for leisure. Young people and adults also need free time to relax
after work or study. Many people spend weekends or holidays doing hobbies, sports, or social activities.
Older people may have more free time after retirement, so they can enjoy leisure activities more regularly.
However, leisure is important for people of all ages because it helps reduce stress and improve mental health.
So, everyone needs leisure time, although different age groups use it differently.

语言学习

 Describe a thing you did to learn another language
 You should say:
 What language you learned
 What you did
 How it helped you learn the language
 And how you felt about it

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“学习类 + 语言类”话题，要求你描述一件你为了学习另一门语言而做的事情。需要说清：你学的是哪种语
言、你做了什么、这件事如何帮助你学习语言，以及你的感受。整体以过去时和现在时结合为主。重点在于学习方
法和实际效果。
思路方面
这里可以选择看视频、练口语、背单词或和外国人交流。思路上可以先说清你学的语言；然后要说清你具体做了什
么；接着可以重点说这个方法是如何帮助你的；最后表达你的感受和收获。
结构方面
第一段：介绍你学习的语言以及你做了什么；
第二段：描述这个方法如何帮助你提高语言能力；
第三段：解释你的感受，并总结这次学习经历。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The language I learned was English. 我学习的语言是英语。
描述感受  I started learning it several years ago. 我几年前开始学习它。
      At first, learning the language was difficult. 一开始学这门语言很困难。
      I wanted to improve my speaking skills. 我想提高口语能力。
      I tried different learning methods. 我尝试了不同学习方法。
      I watched English videos every day. 我每天看英语视频。
      I repeated sentences aloud. 我会大声重复句子。
      I also wrote down useful expressions. 我还会记下有用表达。
      This method improved my listening and speaking. 这个方法提升了我的听力和口语。
      Gradually, I became more confident. 渐渐地，我变得更自信了。
      The learning process was sometimes challenging. 学习过程有时很有挑战。
      However, it was also very rewarding. 不过它也很有成就感。
      I enjoyed seeing my own progress. 我很享受看到自己的进步。
      The experience made language learning more interesting. 这次经历让语言学习更有趣。
      That is why I still continue using this method today. 这就是我现在还在继续用这个方法的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A thing I did to learn another language was watching English videos every day. The language I learned was
English, and I started doing this seriously a few years ago when I wanted to improve my listening and speaking
skills. At first, I found it difficult to understand native speakers because they spoke too quickly for me.
To improve, I began watching short English videos online every evening. I usually chose topics that interested
me, such as travel, daily life, or interviews. While watching, I often repeated sentences aloud and wrote down
useful expressions or new vocabulary. Sometimes I watched the same video several times so I could better
understand the pronunciation and sentence structure. Gradually, this habit helped me become more familiar
with natural English speaking.

63
I think this method helped me a lot because it improved both my listening and speaking at the same time.
Instead of only memorizing grammar rules from textbooks, I could hear how people actually use English in real
conversations. After several months, I noticed that I became more confident when speaking and could
understand videos more easily than before.
Although the learning process was sometimes tiring, I still found it very rewarding. Watching videos made
language learning feel more interesting and practical. It also gave me motivation to continue improving my
English. That is why I still use this method regularly today.

Part 3
Question 1: What difficulties do people face when learning a language?

 解题思路：问题类。可以从“词汇语法”“发音”“缺少练习环境”展开。
Sample Answer:
People face many difficulties when learning a language. Vocabulary and grammar can be hard to remember,
especially when the language structure is very different from their own language.
Pronunciation is another challenge because some sounds may not exist in the learner’s native language. In
addition, many people lack chances to practice speaking in real situations.
So, memory, pronunciation, and limited practice are common problems in language learning.

Question 2: Do you think language learning is important? Why?
 解题思路：观点 + 原因类问题。可以从“沟通”“工作机会”“文化理解”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I think language learning is very important. Learning another language helps people communicate with
others from different countries and cultures.
It can also improve job opportunities, especially in international companies or travel-related work. In addition,
learning a language helps people understand different cultures and ways of thinking.
So, language learning is useful for communication, career development, and personal growth.

Question 3: Which is better, to study a language alone or to study it in a group? Why?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以采取“各有优点，但小组更好”的观点。

Sample Answer:
I think studying a language in a group is usually better because people can practice speaking and
communication together. Group learning also makes lessons more interactive and interesting.
Students can help each other and feel more motivated when learning with classmates. However, studying
alone is also useful for reviewing vocabulary or grammar quietly.
So, both methods are helpful, but group study is often better for improving communication skills.

Question 4: What's the best way to learn a language?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“长期练习”“真实交流”“兴趣结合”展开。

Sample Answer:
I think the best way to learn a language is through regular practice and real communication. Speaking with
other people helps learners improve fluency and confidence more quickly.
Watching videos, reading books, and listening to music in the target language are also helpful because they
make learning more natural and interesting. In addition, learning a little every day is better than studying only
occasionally.
So, consistent practice and real-life use are the most effective ways to learn a language

Place【地】

想有空时去旅游的地方

 Describe a place you would like to visit in your free time
 You should say:
 Where it is
 What you will do there
 How long you will stay there
 And explain why you would like to visit it

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“地点类 + 休闲活动类”话题，要求你描述一个你空闲时间想去的地方。需要说清：这个地方在哪里、你会
在那里做什么、你会待多久，以及你为什么想去那里。整体以将来时为主。重点在于放松感和个人兴趣。
思路方面
这里可以选择自然景点、小城市、海边或安静的地方。思路上可以先说清地点；然后要说清你到了之后会做什么；
接着可以重点说停留时间和生活节奏；最后说明为什么这个地方对你有吸引力。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个地方在哪里；
第二段：描述你会在那里做什么以及会待多久；
第三段：解释你为什么想去那里，并总结感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The place I would like to visit is a seaside town. 我想去的地方是一个海边小镇。
描述感受  It is located in the south of China. 它位于中国南方。
      I have wanted to visit it for a long time. 我想去那里已经很久了。
      It looks quiet and relaxing. 它看起来很安静、很放松。
      I learned about it from travel videos. 我是通过旅行视频了解到它的。
      I would like to walk along the beach. 我想沿着海边散步。
      I also want to try local food. 我还想尝试当地食物。
      I would spend most of my time relaxing. 我大部分时间都会用来放松。
      I plan to stay there for several days. 我计划在那里待几天。
      I want to enjoy the slow pace of life. 我想感受慢节奏生活。
      he place would help me reduce stress. 那个地方能帮我减轻压力。
      I enjoy peaceful environments. 我喜欢安静的环境。
      It feels very different from city life. 它和城市生活很不一样。
      I think the trip would refresh my mind. 我觉得这趟旅行会让我放松很多。
      That is why I want to visit it in my free time. 这就是我想在空闲时间去那里的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A place I would like to visit in my free time is Sanya, which is a famous seaside city in the south of China. I have
wanted to visit this place for a long time because many people say it has beautiful beaches and a very relaxing
atmosphere. I first learned about it from travel videos and photos online.
If I go there, I would spend most of my time near the sea. I would like to walk along the beach, enjoy the fresh
air, and watch the sunset in the evening. I also want to try local seafood and tropical fruit because they look
very fresh and delicious. Besides that, I think I would simply relax in a quiet café or hotel and enjoy the slow
pace of life there. I would probably stay in Sanya for about four or five days, which I think is enough time to rest
properly without feeling rushed.

65
I want to visit this place because it feels very different from my daily life. Normally, my schedule is quite busy,
so I rarely have time to completely relax. A peaceful place like Sanya would help me reduce stress and refresh
my mind. I think spending time near the sea would make me feel calmer and happier. That is why Sanya is a
place I would really like to visit during my free time.

Part 3
Question 1: Why do you think some people choose not to travel abroad?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“费用”“语言与文化差异”“国内旅游替代”三个角度展开，说明不出国旅行并不一
 定是不喜欢旅行。
Sample Answer:
Some people choose not to travel abroad because international travel can be expensive. Flights, hotels, and
visas may cost a lot of money, so domestic travel feels more practical.
Language barriers are another reason. Some people feel uncomfortable in places where they cannot
communicate easily. Cultural differences may also make them nervous.
In addition, some countries have many beautiful places to visit, so people feel there is no need to go abroad.
So, cost, convenience, and personal comfort are the main reasons why some people avoid international travel.

Question 2: Do you think a gap period in life is important?
 解题思路：观点类问题。这里的 gap period 可以理解为 gap year 或人生中的短暂停顿。可以从“放松调整”“思考
 未来”“获得经验”三个角度展开，同时补充“需要合理安排”。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I think a gap period can be important. After long periods of study or work, people may feel tired or unsure
about their future. Taking some time off helps them relax and think more clearly.
During a gap period, people can travel, learn new skills, or gain work experience. These experiences may help
them understand themselves better and make wiser decisions later.
However, the time should be used meaningfully. Without a clear plan, people may waste time. So, a well-
planned gap period can be very valuable for personal growth.

去过的最喜欢的城市

 Describe your favorite city that you have visited
 You should say:
 Where it is
 How you knew it
 When you visited it
 And explain why it is your favourite city

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“地点类 + 旅行经历类”话题，要求你描述一座你去过并且最喜欢的城市。需要说清：这座城市在哪里、你
是怎么知道它的、你什么时候去的，以及为什么它是你最喜欢的城市。整体以过去时描述旅行经历，用现在时表达
现在的感受。重点在于旅行体验和个人感受。
思路方面
这里可以选择国内或国外真实去过的城市。思路上可以先说清城市的位置以及你最初是怎么知道它的；然后要说清
你什么时候去的；接着可以重点说旅行中的体验，比如景点、美食或生活氛围；最后总结为什么它成为你最喜欢的
城市。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这座城市在哪里，以及你是如何知道它的；
第二段：描述你什么时候去的以及在那里做了什么；
第三段：解释为什么这是你最喜欢的城市，并总结感受。

66
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  My favorite city is Kyoto. 我最喜欢的城市是京都。
描述感受  It is located in Japan. 它位于日本。
      I first learned about it from travel programs. 我最早是通过旅游节目了解它的。
      The city has a long history. 这座城市历史悠久。
      Many tourists visit it every year. 每年都有很多游客去那里。
      I visited the city last year. 我去年去了这座城市。
      I stayed there for several days. 我在那里待了几天。
      I visited temples and traditional streets. 我参观了寺庙和传统街道。
      I also tried local food. 我还尝试了当地食物。
      The environment felt peaceful and clean. 那里的环境安静又干净。
      he city made me feel relaxed. 这座城市让我很放松。
      I enjoyed the slow pace of life there. 我很喜欢那里的慢节奏生活。
      It was different from my daily routine. 它和我的日常生活很不一样。
      I would love to visit it again. 我很想再去一次。
      That is why it became my favorite city. 这就是它成为我最喜欢城市的原因。

Sample Answer ：
My favorite city that I have visited is Kyoto. It is located in Japan and is famous for its traditional culture and
beautiful temples. I first learned about this city from travel programs and online videos several years ago. Many
people described it as a peaceful and historic place, which made me very interested in visiting it one day.
I finally visited Kyoto last year during a short holiday. I stayed there for about five days with one of my friends.
During the trip, we visited many famous temples, walked through traditional streets, and tried different kinds
of local food. I especially enjoyed walking around quiet neighborhoods and seeing old wooden houses that
looked very different from buildings in my own city. We also spent some time in small cafés and parks, which
made the whole trip feel slow and relaxing.
Kyoto became my favorite city mainly because of its atmosphere. The city felt peaceful, clean, and well
organized. Compared with my busy daily life, spending time there helped me relax both physically and
mentally. I also liked the way traditional culture and modern life existed together in the city. Everything seemed
calm and meaningful. Even now, I still remember many small details from that trip. That is why Kyoto is my
favorite city that I have ever visited, and I hope I can go there again in the future.

Part 3
Question 1: Which is more suitable for young people, urban life or rural life, and which is more suitable for
old people?

 解题思路：对比类问题。可以从“机会”“生活节奏”“环境”展开。
Sample Answer:
I think urban life is more suitable for young people because cities offer more job opportunities, education, and
entertainment. Young people usually enjoy fast-paced and active lifestyles.
For older people, rural life may be more suitable. The countryside is quieter, less crowded, and closer to nature,
which helps people relax.
However, some elderly people still prefer cities because of better healthcare and public services. So, the best
place depends on personal needs and lifestyle.

Question 2: How do people choose a city to travel to?

 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“兴趣”“预算”“交通便利”展开。
Sample Answer:
People usually choose a city to travel to based on their interests and budget. Some prefer historical cities, while
others enjoy modern cities with shopping and entertainment.
Transportation and accommodation are also important. People often choose places that are easy to reach and
not too expensive. Weather and safety can also influence decisions.

67
So, travel choices depend on personal interests, convenience, and cost.

Question 3: Do you think a tourist city is also a good place to live? Why?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“便利设施”和“生活压力”两个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
A tourist city can be a good place to live because it usually has good transportation, restaurants, and
entertainment facilities. The environment is often clean and attractive as well.
However, tourist cities can also be crowded and expensive. During holidays, traffic and noise may affect local
residents’ daily life.
So, tourist cities offer convenience and opportunities, but they may also bring stress and higher living costs.

Question 4: Do most people prefer traveling to a modern city or a historical city?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以采取“因人而异”的平衡观点。

Sample Answer:
I think different people prefer different types of cities. Some people enjoy modern cities because they offer
shopping, entertainment, and convenient transportation. Modern cities also feel exciting and active.
Others prefer historical cities because they want to learn about culture and history. Old buildings and
traditional streets create a special atmosphere.
So, travel preference depends on personal interests, such as entertainment or cultural experience.

去过的无聊地方

 Describe a boring place
 You should say:
 Where it is
 Who you went there with
 What you did there
 And explain why you think it is a boring place

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“地点类 + 个人体验类”话题，要求你描述一个你觉得无聊的地方。需要说清：这个地方在哪里、你和谁一
起去的、你在那里做了什么，以及为什么你觉得它无聊。整体以过去时为主。重点在于个人感受和具体原因。
思路方面
这里可以选择展览馆、等待区、小城市或无聊的景点。思路上可以先说清地点和同行的人；然后要说清你在那里做
了什么；接着可以重点说让你觉得无聊的原因，比如活动少、环境单调或等待时间太长；最后总结你的感受。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个地方在哪里，以及你和谁一起去；
第二段：描述你在那里做了什么；
第三段：解释为什么你觉得它无聊，并表达你的感受。

68
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The place I want to talk about is a small museum. 我想说的地方是一个小博物馆。
描述感受  It is located in my hometown. 它位于我的家乡。
      I went there with my classmates. 我是和同学一起去的。
      The visit happened during a school activity. 这次参观发生在学校活动期间。
      At first, I thought it would be interesting. 一开始我以为那里会很有趣。
      We walked around the museum slowly. 我们慢慢地参观了博物馆。
      Most of the exhibits looked similar. 大多数展品看起来都差不多。
      There were not many interactive activities. 那里没有太多互动活动。
      I spent a lot of time just reading information. 我花了很多时间只是看介绍。
      The environment felt very quiet and dull. 那里的环境很安静但有些沉闷。
      I lost interest after a short time. 过了一会儿我就失去兴趣了。
      The visit felt much longer than expected. 这次参观看起来比想象中漫长。
      I could not stay focused. 我无法保持专注。
      The place did not leave a strong impression on me. 这个地方没有给我留下深刻印象。
      That is why I think it was a boring place. 这就是我觉得它无聊的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A boring place I once visited was a small museum in my hometown. I went there with several classmates during
a school activity a few years ago. At first, I thought the trip would be interesting because I usually enjoy visiting
new places and learning something different. However, the experience turned out to be much less enjoyable
than I expected.
When we arrived, we walked around the museum slowly and looked at different exhibits. Most of the displays
were old photos, documents, and simple objects placed inside glass cases. Although the information was
probably meaningful, many exhibits looked very similar to each other. There were also very few interactive
activities, so most of the time we were just reading long introductions on the walls. The museum was
extremely quiet, and after some time, I started to feel tired and bored.
I think this place felt boring mainly because the experience was not engaging enough. Everything seemed too
repetitive, and there was nothing that really caught my attention. In addition, we stayed there for quite a long
time, which made the visit feel even longer. Compared with museums that use technology or interactive
displays, this place felt old-fashioned and dull.
Although I understand that museums are important for education and history, this particular one did not leave
a strong impression on me. That is why I still remember it as one of the most boring places I have ever visited.

Part 3
Question 1: Why do most children think education is boring?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“学习方式单一”“压力大”“缺少兴趣关联”展开。

Sample Answer:
Many children think education is boring because lessons are sometimes too focused on memorization and
exams. Repeating information without interaction can make learning feel dull.
Children also have different interests, so they may not enjoy every subject. Too much homework and pressure
can reduce motivation as well.
If classes include games, discussions, or practical activities, children may become more interested. So, teaching
style and pressure strongly affect how children feel about education.

Question 2: Why aren’t young people willing to listen to the experiences of older people?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“代际差异”“独立意识”“社会变化快”展开。

Sample Answer:
Some young people are not willing to listen to older people because they think times have changed. Advice
from older generations may seem old-fashioned or less useful in modern life.

69
Young people also want independence and prefer making their own decisions. In addition, technology and
society change quickly, so younger people may believe their experiences are different.
However, older people still have valuable life experience. So, the problem often comes from communication
gaps between generations.

Question 3: What can people do when they feel bored?
 解题思路：解决方式类问题。可以从“娱乐”“社交”“学习新事物”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
When people feel bored, they can do many different things. Some people relax by watching movies, listening to
music, or playing games. These activities help reduce stress and pass time.
Others prefer meeting friends or doing exercise because social activities improve mood and energy. Some
people also learn new skills or hobbies, such as cooking or drawing.
So, people usually deal with boredom through entertainment, social interaction, or trying something new.

Question 4: Why are some teachers’ classes boring? Are there any solutions?
 解题思路：问题 + 解决类问题。可以从“教学方式单一”“互动不足”“内容脱离兴趣”展开，再给出解决方案。

Sample Answer:
Some teachers’ classes are boring because they focus too much on memorization and long explanations.
Students may lose attention if there is little interaction or discussion.
Another reason is that lessons may not connect with students’ interests or daily life. To improve this, teachers
can use games, videos, or group activities to make lessons more interesting. Asking questions and encouraging
participation also help students stay active.
So, more interactive and practical teaching methods can make classes more enjoyable and effective.

推荐旅行过的地方

 Describe a place you have travelled to that you would like to recommend to others
 You should say:
 What it is
 Where it is
 What you saw and did there
 And explain why you would like to recommend it to others

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“地点类 + 旅行推荐类”话题，要求你描述一个你去过并且想推荐给别人的地方。需要说清：这个地方是什
么、它在哪里、你在那里看到了什么和做了什么，以及你为什么想推荐它。整体以过去时描述旅行经历，用现在时
表达推荐理由。重点在于旅行体验和推荐原因。
思路方面
这里可以选择国内旅游城市、自然景点或海边城市。思路上可以先说清地点和位置；然后要说清你在那里做了什
么、看到了什么；接着可以重点说这个地方的环境、食物或生活节奏；最后总结你为什么推荐它。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个地方是什么以及它在哪里；
第二段：描述你在那里看到和做了什么；
第三段：解释你为什么想推荐这个地方，并表达你的感受。

70
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The place I want to recommend is a coastal city. 我想推荐的地方是一座海滨城市。
描述感受  It is located in the south of China. 它位于中国南方。
      I visited it during a holiday. 我是在假期去那里的。
      The city is famous for its beaches and seafood. 这座城市以海滩和海鲜闻名。
      Many tourists travel there every year. 每年都有很多游客去那里。
      具体描述
      I spent a lot of time near the sea. 我花了很多时间待在海边。
      I walked along the beach every evening. 我每天晚上都会沿着海边散步。
      I also tried different kinds of seafood. 我还尝试了很多不同的海鲜。
      The weather was warm and comfortable. 天气温暖又舒服。
      I enjoyed the slow pace of life there. 我很喜欢那里的慢节奏生活。
      The trip helped me relax completely. 这次旅行让我彻底放松。
      The environment felt peaceful and fresh. 那里的环境安静又清新。
      I think the city is suitable for holidays. 我觉得这座城市很适合度假。
      It gave me many happy memories. 它给了我很多快乐回忆。
      That is why I would recommend it to others. 这就是我会推荐它给别人的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A place I have travelled to that I would like to recommend to others is Sanya. It is a famous coastal city in the
south of China, and many people visit it during holidays because of its beautiful beaches and warm weather. I
travelled there with my family about two years ago during the summer vacation.
During the trip, I spent a lot of time near the sea. Every evening, we walked slowly along the beach and
enjoyed the fresh air and beautiful sunset. I also tried many kinds of local seafood, which tasted very fresh and
delicious. Besides relaxing on the beach, we visited some parks and local markets, where I could see many
tourists and experience the local atmosphere. The city felt lively but not stressful, which made the trip very
enjoyable.
I would recommend this place to others mainly because it is perfect for relaxing. Compared with busy city life,
Sanya has a much slower pace, and the natural scenery makes people feel calm and comfortable. The weather
is warm, the environment is clean, and there are many things to do without feeling rushed. I think it is a good
destination for families, friends, or even people travelling alone. The trip gave me many happy memories, and
that is why I would strongly recommend Sanya to other people.

Part 3
Question 1: Where do people in your country often go for holidays?

 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“自然景点”“大城市”“海外旅行”几个方向展开。
Sample Answer:
People in my country often travel to natural places during holidays, such as beaches, mountains, or lakes. These
places help people relax and escape busy city life.
Some people prefer visiting famous cities because they enjoy shopping, food, and entertainment. Historical
cities are also popular among tourists who want to learn about culture and history.
In recent years, more people have started traveling abroad as well. So, travel choices depend on people’s
interests and budgets.

Question 2: What is the ideal length for a holiday?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以给出一个合理时间，并解释原因。
Sample Answer:
I think one or two weeks is an ideal length for a holiday. This gives people enough time to relax, travel, and
recover from stress without feeling rushed.
A very short holiday may not help people rest properly, while a very long holiday can make people lose their
daily routine or feel bored.

71
So, a balanced holiday length is best because it allows relaxation while still keeping life organized.

Question 3: How do people usually plan holidays?
 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“预算”“地点”“网络工具”展开。

Sample Answer:
People usually begin planning holidays by deciding their budget and travel dates. After that, they choose
destinations based on interests such as beaches, history, or shopping.
Many people use the internet to compare hotels, flights, and tourist information. Some also make schedules
for activities and transportation before traveling.
So, holiday planning often includes budgeting, destination choice, and online research.

Question 4: Is it important to plan a holiday?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“节省时间”“控制预算”“减少问题”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I think planning a holiday is important. Good planning helps people manage their budget, transportation,
and accommodation more easily.
Planning also reduces stress because people know what to do and where to go in advance. During busy travel
seasons, booking early can save money and avoid problems. However, some flexibility is also useful during
travel.
So, holiday planning helps people travel more smoothly and comfortably.

喜欢拜访但不想住的家

 Describe a home that you like to visit but do not want to live in
 You should say:
 Where it is
 What it is like
 Why you like to visit it
 And explain why you would not like to live there

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“地点类 + 个人偏好类”话题，要求你描述一个你喜欢去但不想长期居住的家。需要说清：它在哪里、是什
么样子、你为什么喜欢去，以及为什么不想住在那里。整体以现在时为主。重点在于喜欢与不适合长期居住之间的
对比。
思路方面
这里可以选择亲戚、朋友或乡下的房子。思路上可以先说清这个家的位置和外观；然后要说清你为什么喜欢去，比
如环境好、氛围轻松；接着可以重点说为什么不适合长期住，比如交通不方便、空间太小或生活节奏不同；最后总
结感受。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个家在哪里以及它是什么样子；
第二段：描述你为什么喜欢去那里；
第三段：解释为什么你不想长期住在那里，并总结感受

72
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The home I want to talk about belongs to my grandparents. 我想说的房子属于我爷爷奶奶。
描述感受  It is located in the countryside. 它位于乡下。
      The house has a very traditional style. 这栋房子是很传统的风格。
      It is surrounded by nature. 它被自然环境包围。
      I usually visit it during holidays. 我通常假期会去那里。
      The environment there is peaceful and quiet. 那里的环境安静平和。
      I enjoy spending time in the yard. 我喜欢待在院子里。
      The air feels very fresh. 那里的空气很新鲜。
      The house gives me a relaxing feeling. 这栋房子让我感到放松。
      I always feel comfortable when visiting. 我每次去都觉得很舒服。
      However, life there is not very convenient. 不过那里的生活不是很方便。
      There are fewer shops and services nearby. 附近商店和服务比较少。
      Transportation is also limited. 交通也有限。
      I prefer the convenience of city life. 我更喜欢城市生活的便利。
      That is why I enjoy visiting but would not want to live there permanently. 这就是我喜欢去但不想长期
      住那里的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A home that I like to visit but do not want to live in is my grandparents’ house in the countryside. It is located in
a small village outside the city, and I usually go there during holidays or family gatherings. The house is quite
traditional and surrounded by trees, plants, and open fields, which makes the environment feel very peaceful.
I enjoy visiting this home because it is very different from my daily life in the city. The air is fresh, the streets
are quiet, and everything feels much slower and more relaxing. Whenever I go there, I like sitting in the yard,
talking with my grandparents, and enjoying home-cooked food. Sometimes I also take short walks around the
village because the natural scenery is beautiful and calming. Compared with the noisy city, the atmosphere
there helps me relax a lot.
However, although I enjoy visiting this place, I would not want to live there permanently. The main reason is
that life in the countryside is less convenient. There are fewer shops, restaurants, and entertainment options
nearby, and transportation is not very convenient either. In addition, I am already used to the fast pace and
convenience of city life. I think staying there for a short holiday is wonderful, but living there every day might
become a little boring for me.
That is why I like visiting my grandparents’ home but would not want to live there permanently.

Part 3
Question 1: Do Chinese people like to visit others’ homes?

 解题思路：文化类问题。可以从“家庭观念”“社交习惯”“现代变化”展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, many Chinese people like visiting others’ homes, especially during festivals or family gatherings. Visiting
relatives and friends is an important part of social culture in China.
People often bring small gifts or food when visiting someone’s home. However, younger people today may
meet more often in restaurants or cafés because modern life is busy.
So, home visits are still common in China, although social habits are slowly changing.

Question 2: What do Chinese people do when they visit others?

 解题思路：文化类问题。可以从“带礼物”“聊天吃饭”“表达礼貌”展开。
Sample Answer:
When Chinese people visit others, they often bring small gifts such as fruit, snacks, or tea to show politeness
and respect.
Hosts usually prepare food or drinks, and people spend time chatting together. During festivals or family visits,
meals are often an important part of the gathering.

73
So, visiting others in China is usually warm and polite, with a strong focus on hospitality and social connection.

Question 3: What kind of place do people in your country like to live in?
 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“便利性”“环境”“不同年龄偏好”展开。

Sample Answer:
Many people in my country like living in places with convenient transportation, good public services, and a
comfortable environment. Young people often prefer cities because of work opportunities and entertainment.
Older people may prefer quieter places with cleaner air and less stress. Some families also like living near
parks, schools, or shopping areas because daily life becomes easier.
So, people usually choose places that balance convenience, comfort, and lifestyle needs.

Question 4: What's the difference between homes in cities and those in the countryside?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以从“空间”“环境”“生活便利”展开。

Sample Answer:
Homes in cities are usually smaller and closer together because land is expensive. However, city homes are
often more convenient because they are near schools, shops, and public transportation.
Homes in the countryside are generally larger and quieter. People there often have gardens or open space
around their houses. The environment is usually more peaceful and natural.
So, city homes offer convenience, while countryside homes provide more space and a calmer lifestyle.

重要河流/湖泊

 Describe an important river/lake in your country
 You should say:
 Where it is located
 How big/long it is
 What it looks like
 And explain why it is important

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“地点类 + 地理类”话题，要求你描述一个你国家里重要的河流或湖泊。需要说清：它在哪里、它有多大或
多长、它看起来是什么样子，以及它为什么重要。整体以现在时为主。重点在于自然特点和实际意义。
思路方面
这里可以选择中国著名河流或湖泊，比如长江、西湖或洞庭湖。思路上可以先说清它的位置；然后要说清规模和外
观；接着可以重点说它的重要性，比如历史、经济或旅游价值；最后表达你的看法。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这个河流或湖泊的位置；
第二段：描述它的大小和外观；
第三段：解释它为什么重要，并表达你的感受。

74
背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The river I want to talk about is the Yangtze River. 我想说的河流是长江。
描述感受  It is located in China. 它位于中国。
      It is one of the most famous rivers in the country. 它是中国最著名的河流之一。
      Many cities are built along the river. 很多城市都建在江边。
      The river has a long history. 这条河历史悠久。
      It is extremely long and wide. 它非常长也非常宽。
      The river passes through many areas. 这条河流经很多地区。
      The scenery around it is beautiful. 它周围风景很美。
      In some places, the water looks calm and peaceful. 有些地方水面平静安宁。
      The river changes a lot in different seasons. 这条河在不同季节变化很大。
      The river is important for transportation and farming. 这条河对交通和农业很重要。
      It supports the lives of many people. 它支撑着很多人的生活。
      It also attracts many tourists every year. 它每年也吸引很多游客。
      I think it is an important symbol of China. 我觉得它是中国的重要象征。
      That is why this river is so important. 这就是这条河如此重要的原因。

Sample Answer ：
An important river in my country is Yangtze River. It is one of the most famous rivers in China and is located
across several parts of the country. The river starts in western China and flows all the way to the eastern coast.
Many large cities are built along it, so it plays a very important role in people’s daily lives.
The Yangtze River is extremely long and wide. In fact, it is the longest river in China and one of the longest
rivers in the world. The scenery around the river changes a lot depending on the area. In some places, there
are mountains and forests nearby, while in other places, there are busy cities and large bridges crossing the
river. Sometimes the water looks calm and peaceful, especially during good weather, and the natural scenery
can be very beautiful.
I think this river is important for several reasons. First, it supports transportation, farming, and business
activities in many regions. Second, millions of people depend on it for water and economic development. In
addition, the river has strong historical and cultural importance in China, so many people see it as an important
national symbol. It also attracts many tourists every year because of its beautiful scenery.
That is why I believe the Yangtze River is one of the most important rivers in my country.

Part 3
Question 1: Are rivers and lakes important to a country?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“资源”“经济”“环境”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, rivers and lakes are very important to a country. They provide water for daily life, farming, and industry.
Many cities also depend on rivers for transportation and economic activities.
Rivers and lakes are important for the environment as well. They support wildlife and help maintain natural
balance. In addition, beautiful lakes and rivers can attract tourists.
So, rivers and lakes are valuable for both economic development and environmental protection.

Question 2: What are the popular water sports in your country?

 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“游泳”“冲浪”“划船”等常见项目展开。

Sample Answer:
Swimming is one of the most popular water sports in my country because it is healthy and suitable for many
ages. People often swim in pools during summer.
In coastal areas, sports such as surfing and sailing are becoming more popular among young people. Some
people also enjoy boating or kayaking during holidays.
So, water sports are becoming more common because they are fun, relaxing, and good for health.

75
Question 3: Are rivers and lakes good for transport? Why?
 解题思路：观点 + 原因类问题。可以从“运输成本”“大量货物”“环境影响”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, rivers and lakes are good for transport in many situations. Water transport is usually cheaper than road or
air transport, especially for large amounts of goods.
It can also reduce traffic pressure in busy cities and may be better for the environment because ships use less
energy for heavy transport. However, water transport is slower and depends on weather conditions.
So, rivers and lakes are useful for transportation, especially for heavy goods and long-distance shipping.
Question 4: How do rivers and lakes affect local tourism?

 解题思路：影响类问题。可以从“自然景观”“旅游活动”“经济带动”展开。
Sample Answer:
Rivers and lakes can strongly support local tourism. Beautiful natural scenery attracts visitors who want to
relax, take photos, or enjoy outdoor activities.
Many places also offer water sports, boat tours, or fishing activities, which make tourism more interesting.
Hotels and restaurants near rivers and lakes can benefit from more visitors as well.
So, rivers and lakes help tourism by improving natural beauty and creating recreational opportunities.

Object【物品】

喜欢或不喜欢的高建筑

 Describe a tall building you like or dislike
 You should say:
 What it is used for
 Where it is
 What it looks like
 And explain why you like or dislike it

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“地点类 + 建筑类”话题，要求你描述一栋你喜欢或不喜欢的高楼。需要说清：这栋楼是做什么用的、它在
哪里、外观是什么样子，以及你为什么喜欢或不喜欢它。整体以现在时为主。重点在于建筑特点 + 个人感受。
思路方面
这里可以选择地标建筑、办公楼或酒店。思路上可以先说清这栋楼的位置和用途；然后要说清它的外观，比如高
度、颜色或设计风格；接着可以重点说你为什么喜欢它，比如景观、设计感或城市氛围。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这栋高楼是什么以及它在哪里；
第二段：描述它的用途和外观；
第三段：解释你为什么喜欢它，并总结感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The building I want to talk about is a famous tower. 我想说的建筑是一座著名高塔。
描述感受  It is located in a busy city center. 它位于繁忙的市中心。
      Many tourists visit it every year. 每年都有很多游客去那里。
      It is one of the tallest buildings in the area. 它是当地最高的建筑之一。
      I first saw it in a travel program. 我最早是在旅游节目里看到它的。
      The building is mainly used for offices and tourism. 这栋楼主要用于办公和旅游。
      It has a modern glass design. 它是现代玻璃设计。
      The tower looks very impressive at night. 这座塔在夜晚看起来非常震撼。
      Its lights can be seen from far away. 很远就能看到它的灯光。
      The building stands out from the surrounding area. 它在周围建筑中非常显眼。
      I like its modern appearance. 我喜欢它现代化的外观。
      It gives the city a special atmosphere. 它让城市更有特色。
      I enjoy looking at it from a distance. 我喜欢远远地看着它。
      The design makes me feel amazed. 它的设计让我感到惊叹。
      That is why I like this tall building so much. 这就是我喜欢这栋高楼的原因。

Sample Answer ：
A tall building that I really like is Shanghai Tower. It is located in the center of Shanghai, one of the biggest cities
in China. This building is very famous because it is one of the tallest buildings in the world, and many tourists
visit it every year.
The building is mainly used for offices, hotels, and tourism. It has a very modern design with a glass exterior,
and the shape of the tower looks slightly twisted as it goes upward. During the daytime, the building reflects
sunlight and looks bright and clean. At night, the lights make it look even more impressive. Because it is so tall,
people can see it from many different parts of the city. There is also an observation deck where visitors can
enjoy the view of Shanghai from high above.

77
I like this building mainly because of its modern appearance and strong visual impact. Whenever I see photos
or videos of it, I feel amazed by how advanced and beautiful it looks. I also think it represents the fast
development of big cities in China. Compared with ordinary buildings, it gives the city a more exciting and
international atmosphere. That is why Shanghai Tower is a tall building that I truly like and hope to visit again in
the future.

Part 3
Question 1: Are there many tall buildings in your country?

 解题思路：事实 + 说明类问题。可以从“城市化发展”“人口密度”“现代化象征”三个角度展开，解释为什么高楼在

 中国越来越常见。
Sample Answer:
Yes, there are many tall buildings in my country, especially in large cities. As cities develop quickly, more people
move to urban areas, so tall buildings help save space and provide housing and offices.
Many modern business areas are full of skyscrapers, which also show economic development and modern city
life. In addition, shopping malls and hotels are often built in tall buildings.
However, smaller cities and rural areas usually have fewer tall buildings. So, tall buildings are very common in
big cities, but less common in quieter areas.

Question 2: What are the differences between those tall buildings in your country?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以从“功能”“设计风格”“地理位置”三个方面展开，说明不同高楼的用途和特点不同，

 而不是只比较高度
Sample Answer:
Tall buildings in my country are different in many ways. Some are office buildings used for business, while
others are apartment buildings for living. Their designs and functions are not the same.
In big business areas, tall buildings often look modern and use glass and steel. Residential buildings are usually
simpler and focus more on comfort and daily life. Some tall buildings are also hotels or shopping centers.
The location also affects their style. Buildings in large cities are usually taller and more modern than those in
smaller cities. So, purpose and location create many differences among tall buildings.

Question 3: Why are different places laid out and designed differently?
 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“自然环境”“文化背景”“功能需求”三个方面展开，说明不同地区为什么会形成不

 同的设计风格和布局。
Sample Answer:
Different places are designed differently because they serve different needs. Climate is one important reason.
For example, hot places may need open spaces and better airflow, while colder areas require warmer and more
closed buildings.
Culture also affects design. Some countries prefer traditional styles, while others focus more on modern and
practical designs. In addition, the purpose of a place matters. Business areas, parks, and residential
neighborhoods all need different layouts.
So, environment, culture, and function are the main reasons why places are planned differently.

Question 4: What are the advantages of living in tall buildings?
 解题思路：优势类问题。可以从“节省空间”“生活便利”“视野与安全”三个角度展开，说明高层住宅在现代城市中

 的实用性。
Sample Answer:
Living in tall buildings has several advantages. First, tall buildings save space, which is very important in
crowded cities. More people can live in the same area, so land can be used more efficiently.
Second, tall buildings are often located in convenient areas close to shops, schools, and public transportation.
This makes daily life easier.
People living on higher floors may also enjoy better views and less street noise. Some modern buildings also
provide security systems and shared facilities such as gyms or gardens.
So, tall buildings offer convenience, efficient use of space, and comfortable city living.

78
收到特殊蛋糕

 Describe a special cake you received from others
 You should say:
 When it happened
 Where it happened
 Who you got the cake from
 And explain why it's a special cake

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“经历类 + 礼物类”话题，要求你描述一个别人送给你的特别蛋糕。需要说清：事情发生在什么时候、在哪
里、是谁送给你的，以及为什么这个蛋糕特别。整体以过去时为主，最后可以用现在时总结感受。重点在于情感意
义和当时的氛围。
思路方面
这里可以选择生日、毕业、节日或惊喜聚会中的蛋糕。思路上可以先说清事情发生的时间和地点；然后要说清是谁
送给你的；接着可以重点说蛋糕特别在哪里，比如外形、味道或背后的心意；最后表达你的感受。
结构方面
第一段：介绍事情发生的时间、地点和送蛋糕的人；
第二段：描述蛋糕的样子和当时发生的事情；
第三段：解释为什么这个蛋糕很特别，并总结感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  It happened on my birthday last year. 事情发生在去年我生日的时候。
描述感受  The celebration took place at home. 庆祝是在家里进行的。
      I received the cake from my friends. 我从朋友那里收到了这个蛋糕。
      It was a complete surprise. 这是一个完全的惊喜。
      I did not expect anything special that day. 那天我原本没期待什么特别的事。
      The cake had a simple but beautiful design. 蛋糕设计简单但很好看。
      It was decorated with fruit and chocolate. 它用了水果和巧克力装饰。
      My name was written on the top. 上面写着我的名字。
      Everyone sang a birthday song together. 大家一起唱了生日歌。
      The atmosphere was warm and cheerful. 当时的气氛很温暖开心。
      I felt touched and happy. 我感到感动又开心。
      The cake showed their care for me. 这个蛋糕体现了他们对我的关心。
      It became one of my favorite memories. 它成了我最喜欢的回忆之一。
      I still remember that moment clearly. 我现在还清楚记得那个时刻。
      That is why the cake was very special to me. 这就是为什么那个蛋糕对我来说很特别。

Sample Answer ：

A special cake I received from others was on my birthday last year. The celebration happened at my home in
the evening, and the cake was given to me by a group of my close friends. I did not expect anything special that
day, because I thought everyone was too busy with study and work.
That evening, my friends suddenly came to my house with a birthday cake and some small gifts. The cake was
not very large, but it had a beautiful design. It was decorated with fruit, cream, and chocolate, and my name
was written on the top. What made it even more special was that they chose my favorite flavor, which was
mango. After they brought out the cake, everyone sang a birthday song together, and the atmosphere became
very warm and cheerful.

79
I think this cake was special not because of its price or appearance, but because of the feelings behind it. My
friends spent time planning the surprise and remembered what I liked, which made me feel truly cared for. At
that moment, I felt very touched and grateful to have such thoughtful friends around me. Even now, I still
remember that evening clearly whenever I think about birthdays. That is why this cake became one of the most
special cakes I have ever received.

Part 3
Question 1: What are the differences between special food in China and other countries?

 解题思路：对比类问题。可以从“口味”“烹饪方式”“饮食文化”三个方面展开，不需要绝对化，而是强调文化差
 异。
Sample Answer:
Chinese special food is often very different from food in other countries. Chinese dishes usually have rich
flavors and use many cooking methods, such as frying, steaming, and boiling. Sharing food together is also an
important part of Chinese culture.
In some other countries, food may be simpler in flavor and served in separate portions. People may focus more
on bread, meat, or cold dishes.
So, the main differences are in taste, cooking style, and eating habits, which are closely connected to culture
and history.

Question 2: Is there any food in your country that is eaten at special times or on special occasions?
 解题思路：说明与举例类问题。可以结合中国节日文化展开，重点解释“食物与意义”的联系。

Sample Answer:
Yes, there are many foods in my country that are eaten on special occasions. For example, people eat
dumplings during the Chinese New Year because they represent wealth and good luck.
Mooncakes are popular during the Mid-Autumn Festival, when families gather together and enjoy the full
moon. Rice dumplings are eaten during the Dragon Boat Festival to remember an important historical person.
These foods are not only for eating. They also carry cultural meaning and help families celebrate together. So,
special foods are an important part of traditional festivals in my country.

Question 3: Why are some people willing to spend a lot of money on meals on special days?
 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“纪念意义”“社交情感”“体验价值”三个角度展开。

Sample Answer:
Some people spend a lot of money on meals on special days because these occasions are important to them.
Birthdays, weddings, and festivals are meaningful moments, so people want to celebrate properly.
A good meal also creates a warm atmosphere for family and friends. Many people think the experience and
memories are more valuable than the money itself. In addition, eating at a special restaurant can make the day
feel more exciting.
So, emotions, celebration, and shared experiences make people willing to pay more for meals on important
occasions.

Question 4: Do you think it’s good to communicate when eating with your family?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“增进感情”“分享信息”“减少距离感”三个角度展开，强调家庭交流的重要性。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I think it is very good to communicate when eating with family. Mealtime gives family members a chance
to talk about daily life, school, or work. This helps people understand and support each other better.
Talking during meals also creates a warm and relaxed atmosphere. In modern life, many people are busy, so
family meals may be one of the few times they spend together.
So, communication during meals is important for building strong family relationships and improving emotional
connection.

特别场合的食物

 Describe a food that people eat on special occasions or events
 You should say:
 What it is
 What the special event or occasion is
 How it is cooked or made
 And explain why people eat it on that special occasion or event

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“物品类+食物类”话题，要求你描述一种人们在特殊场合会吃的食物。需要说清：这是什么食物、在什么
场合吃、如何制作，以及为什么人们会在这个场合吃它。整体以现在时为主。重点在于食物背后的文化意义和习
俗。
思路方面
这里可以选择节日食品、生日食品或家庭聚会常见食物。思路上可以先说清食物名称和对应场合；然后要说清基本
制作过程；接着可以重点说这种食物的象征意义；最后说明为什么人们会在这个特殊场合吃它。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这种食物以及对应的特殊场合；
第二段：描述它是如何制作的；
第三段：解释为什么人们会在这个场合吃它，并表达你的感受。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The food I want to talk about is dumplings. 我想说的食物是饺子。
描述感受  People often eat it during the Spring Festival. 人们通常在春节吃它。
      It is a traditional Chinese food. 它是一种传统中国食物。
      Many families make it together. 很多家庭会一起做。
      The dish is closely connected to family gatherings. 这道食物和家庭团聚关系密切。
      The filling is usually made with meat and vegetables. 馅料通常由肉和蔬菜做成。
      People wrap the filling in thin dough. 人们会把馅包进面皮里。
      Then the dumplings are boiled in hot water. 然后把饺子放进热水里煮。
      Different families have different recipes. 不同家庭有不同做法。
      Making dumplings often takes time and teamwork. 包饺子通常需要时间和合作。
      The food represents reunion and happiness. 这种食物象征团圆和幸福。
      Eating it creates a warm atmosphere. 吃它会营造温暖氛围。
      It reminds people of family traditions. 它让人想到家庭传统。
      I always look forward to eating it. 我总是很期待吃它。
      That is why this food is important on special occasions. 这就是这种食物在特殊场合重要的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A food that people often eat on special occasions in China is dumplings. It is a very traditional Chinese food,
and many families eat it during the Spring Festival, which is the most important holiday in China. During this
festival, family members usually gather together, have dinner, and celebrate the beginning of a new year.
Dumplings are normally made with thin dough and different kinds of fillings. The filling is often prepared with
meat and vegetables, such as pork, cabbage, or green onions. After preparing the filling, people wrap it
carefully inside small pieces of dough. Then the dumplings are boiled in hot water until they are ready to eat. In
many families, making dumplings is not only about cooking food, but also about spending time together.
Everyone sits around the table and helps with the process.

81
People eat dumplings on special occasions because they represent reunion, happiness, and good luck. Their
shape is also believed to look like ancient Chinese gold pieces, so many people think eating dumplings can
bring wealth and good fortune in the new year. For me, dumplings are more than just food. Whenever I eat
them during the Spring Festival, I feel a strong sense of warmth and family connection. That is why this food is
so important during special celebrations in China.

Part 3
Question 1: Why are there special foods on special occasions or events?

 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“文化传统”“象征意义”“家庭团聚”三个方面展开。
Sample Answer:
There are special foods on special occasions because food is closely connected to culture and tradition. Many
traditional foods have symbolic meanings, such as good luck, happiness, or family unity.
Special food also helps create a festive atmosphere. During holidays or celebrations, families and friends gather
together and enjoy meals as part of the event. These traditions are often passed down from older generations.
So, special foods are important because they carry cultural meaning and help people celebrate together.

Question 2: What are the differences between everyday food and festival food?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以从“制作复杂度”“象征意义”“家庭氛围”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Everyday food is usually simple and practical because people eat it regularly. It is often quick to prepare and
focuses on daily nutrition and convenience.
Festival food is different because it carries cultural meaning and is often prepared more carefully. Special dishes
are usually richer and more expensive, and families often cook together during festivals. Some foods also
symbolize good luck or happiness.
So, everyday food focuses on daily needs, while festival food is more connected to tradition, celebration, and
family gatherings

Question 3: Are there any differences between the food people eat today and the food people ate in the
past?

 解题思路：对比类问题。可以从“饮食种类”“烹饪方式”“生活节奏”三个方面展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, there are many differences between food today and food in the past. In the past, people usually ate
simpler and more traditional meals made with local ingredients. Home cooking was more common.
Today, people have more food choices because of globalization and technology. Fast food and takeout are
much more popular now because modern life is busy. People also pay more attention to healthy food and
nutrition.
So, modern food is more diverse and convenient, while traditional food was usually simpler and more
connected to local culture.

包含动物的故事或书

 Describe a story/book with animals in it
 You should say:
 What animals are in it
 What the story/book is about
 Why you read the story/book
 And explain what you think of this story/book

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“书籍类 + 故事类”话题，要求你描述一本有动物角色的故事书。需要说清：里面有哪些动物、故事讲了什
么、你为什么读这本书，以及你对这本书的看法。整体以现在时和过去时结合为主。重点在于故事内容和个人感
受。
思路方面
这里可以选择童话、寓言或儿童文学。思路上可以先说清故事中的动物；然后要说清故事主要内容；接着可以重点
说你为什么会读这本书；最后表达你对这个故事的看法，比如有趣、感人或有教育意义。
结构方面
第一段：介绍这本书以及里面有哪些动物；
第二段：描述故事内容和你为什么会读它；
第三段：解释你对这个故事的感受，并总结原因。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  The story I want to talk about is a famous children’s book. 我想说的故事是一本著名儿童书。
描述感受  It includes several animal characters. 它里面有几个动物角色。
      I read it when I was younger. 我小时候读过它。
      The book is popular in many countries. 这本书在很多国家都很受欢迎。
      I first learned about it at school. 我最早是在学校知道它的。
      The main characters are different farm animals. 主要角色是不同的农场动物。
      The story is about friendship and teamwork. 这个故事关于友谊和合作。
      Each animal has a different personality. 每只动物都有不同性格。
      The animals go through many challenges together. 这些动物一起经历了很多困难。
      The story is simple but meaningful. 这个故事简单但很有意义。
      I found the book very enjoyable. 我觉得这本书非常有趣。
      The story taught me some important lessons. 这个故事教会了我一些重要道理。
      I liked the animal characters a lot. 我很喜欢里面的动物角色。
      The book left a strong impression on me. 这本书给我留下了深刻印象。
      That is why I still remember it clearly today. 这就是我今天还清楚记得它的原因。

Sample Answer ：

A story with animals in it that I still remember clearly is Charlotte's Web. It is a famous children’s book that
includes several animal characters, especially a pig named Wilbur and a spider named Charlotte. I first read this
book when I was in primary school after my teacher recommended it to our class.
The story is mainly about friendship and kindness. Wilbur is a small pig living on a farm, and one day he learns
that he may be killed when he grows bigger. Charlotte, the spider, decides to help him by creating words in her
web to make people think Wilbur is special. Because of her efforts, Wilbur becomes famous and is eventually
saved. Besides Wilbur and Charlotte, there are also other farm animals in the story, and each one has a
different personality.

83
I read this book because many people said it was touching and meaningful, and I was curious about it. After
reading it, I understood why it is so popular. Although the story is simple, it teaches important lessons about
friendship, loyalty, and helping others. I especially liked the relationship between the pig and the spider
because it showed that true friendship can exist between very different individuals.
I think this is a wonderful story because it is emotional, easy to understand, and meaningful for both children
and adults. That is why I still remember it clearly today.

Part 3
Question 1: Should schools teach children about animals?

 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“自然知识”“环保意识”“兴趣培养”展开。
Sample Answer:
Yes, schools should teach children about animals. Learning about animals helps children understand nature and
the environment better. It can also increase respect and care for living things.
Children are usually interested in animals, so these lessons can make learning more enjoyable. Schools can also
teach students about protecting endangered animals and natural habitats.
So, animal education is useful for both knowledge and environmental awareness.

Question 2: Some people think pets should not be kept in cities. What do you think?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以采取平衡观点，承认问题但认为合理管理即可。

Sample Answer:
I think pets can be kept in cities if owners are responsible. Pets can provide emotional support and reduce
loneliness, especially for people living alone.
However, there can be problems such as noise, safety issues, or dirty public spaces if pets are not managed
properly. Owners should follow rules and take good care of their animals.
So, pets are acceptable in cities as long as people behave responsibly.

Question 3: Many people regard pets as members of their family. What do you think?
 解题思路：观点类问题。可以从“情感陪伴”“家庭关系”“责任”展开。

Sample Answer:
I think it is natural for many people to regard pets as family members. Pets provide emotional support and
companionship, especially for people who live alone or feel stressed.
Many owners spend a lot of time caring for their pets, so strong emotional bonds develop over time. However,
keeping pets also means taking responsibility for their health and behavior.
So, pets can become part of the family because they bring comfort, friendship, and emotional connection.

Question 4: Do many people keep pets in your country?
 解题思路：现象类问题。可以从“城市养宠增加”“陪伴需求”“常见宠物”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, many people in my country keep pets nowadays, especially in cities. Dogs and cats are the most common
pets because they provide companionship and emotional support.
Young people and older adults often enjoy having pets at home. In recent years, keeping pets has become
more popular because living standards have improved and people pay more attention to emotional well-being.
So, pet ownership is becoming increasingly common in my country.

84
近期改变的计划

 Describe a plan that you had to change recently
 You should say:
 When this happened
 What made you change the plan
 What the new plan was
 And how you felt about the change

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“经历类 + 变化调整类”话题，要求你描述一个你最近不得不改变的计划。需要说清：这件事是什么时候发
生的、为什么你要改变计划、新计划是什么，以及你对这个变化的感受。整体以过去时为主。重点在于计划变化的
原因和你的情绪变化。
思路方面
这里可以选择旅行计划、学习安排或聚会。思路上可以先说清原本的计划是什么；然后要说清是什么原因导致计划
改变；接着可以重点说新的安排；最后表达你从失望到接受甚至满意的情绪变化。
结构方面
第一段：介绍原本的计划以及事情发生的时间；
第二段：描述为什么计划发生变化，以及新的安排；
第三段：解释你对这次变化的感受，并总结想法。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  This happened about a month ago. 这件事发生在大约一个月前。
描述感受  I originally planned to travel with my friends. 我原本计划和朋友去旅行。
      We had prepared for it in advance. 我们提前做了准备。
      Everything seemed well organized at first. 一开始一切看起来都安排好了。
      I was looking forward to the plan. 我当时很期待这个计划。
      Something unexpected happened. 发生了一些意外情况。
      The weather suddenly became terrible. 天气突然变得很糟。
      We had to cancel the original plan. 我们不得不取消原计划。
      Instead, we changed it to a short local trip. 后来我们改成了本地短途活动。
      The new plan was simpler and more practical. 新计划更简单也更现实。
      At first, I felt disappointed. 一开始我很失望。
      Later, I understood the situation better. 后来我更能理解当时的情况。
      The change turned out to be a good idea. 这个改变后来证明是个好主意。
      I learned to be more flexible. 我学会了更加灵活。
      That experience left a strong impression on me. 那次经历给我留下了深刻印象。

Sample Answer ：

A plan that I had to change recently happened about a month ago. My friends and I originally planned to take a
short trip to another city during a weekend holiday. We had already discussed the transportation, hotel, and
places we wanted to visit, so I was really looking forward to it.
However, a few days before the trip, the weather suddenly became very bad. Heavy rain was expected for
several days, and there were also delays in public transportation. Because of these problems, we realized that
continuing with the original plan would not be safe or enjoyable. After discussing it together, we decided to
cancel the trip and change it to a simple local activity instead. In the end, we spent the weekend visiting a
nearby park, eating together, and watching a movie.

85
At first, I felt quite disappointed because I had been excited about traveling for a long time. I even prepared
clothes and made a list of places I wanted to visit. However, after spending time with my friends during the
new plan, I slowly felt better. The experience was still relaxing and enjoyable, even though it was different from
what I expected.
This change taught me that plans do not always go perfectly, and sometimes being flexible is important.
Looking back, I think changing the plan was actually a smart decision, and I learned how to deal with
unexpected situations more calmly.

Part 3
Question 1: Do people often change their plans?

 解题思路：现象类问题。可以从“生活变化快”“突发情况”“个人想法改变”解释。
Sample Answer:
Yes, people often change their plans today. Modern life changes quickly, so unexpected situations happen quite
often. Work schedules, traffic, or family matters may force people to adjust plans.
People may also change their minds after getting new information or ideas. Sometimes changing a plan is a
practical choice rather than a problem.
So, changing plans is very common because life is flexible and unpredictable.

Question 2: Would you tell others if you change your plan?
 解题思路：个人态度类问题。可以根据“是否影响他人”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, I would usually tell others if I change my plan, especially when the plan involves other people. Informing
others shows respect and helps avoid misunderstanding or inconvenience.
For example, if I cancel a meeting or change travel time, I think it is important to let people know early.
However, if the change only affects myself, I may not always mention it.
So, whether I tell others depends on how much the change influences them.

Question 3: Why do you think parents still make plans for their children nowadays?
 解题思路：原因类问题。可以从“竞争压力”“对子女未来的担心”“传统观念”展开。

Sample Answer:
Parents still make plans for their children because they care about their future. Competition in education and
work is strong, so parents want to help children succeed.
Many parents also believe their experience can guide children and help them avoid mistakes. In some cultures,
parents feel responsible for making important decisions for the family.
So, concern for children’s future and social pressure are the main reasons parents continue making plans for
them.

Question 4: How does technology help people make plans?
 解题思路：影响类问题。可以从“信息获取”“时间管理”“沟通协调”三个方面展开。

Sample Answer:
Technology helps people make plans more easily and efficiently. Mobile phones and apps allow people to
organize schedules, set reminders, and manage time clearly.
The internet also provides useful information about weather, transportation, or prices, which helps people
make better decisions. In addition, communication tools make it easier for groups to discuss and change plans
quickly.
So, technology improves planning by saving time and making information easier to access.

有趣视频

 Describe an interesting video
 You should say:
 When and where you watched it
 What it is about
 Why you watched it
 And explain how you feel about it

教师的解析：
审题方面
这是一道“媒体类 + 经历类”话题，要求你描述一个你觉得很有趣的视频。需要说清：你什么时候、在哪里看的，视
频内容是什么，你为什么会看它，以及你对这个视频的感受。整体以过去时描述观看经历，用现在时表达感受。重
点在于视频内容和你的兴趣点。
思路方面
这里可以选择纪录片、旅行视频、科普视频或短视频。思路上可以先说清你是在什么情况下看到这个视频的；然后
要说清视频主要讲了什么；接着可以重点说你为什么点开它；最后说明这个视频为什么让你觉得有趣。
结构方面
第一段：介绍你什么时候、在哪里看到了这个视频；
第二段：描述视频内容以及你为什么会看它；
第三段：解释你对这个视频的感受，并总结原因。

背景描述                                           答题参考
具体描述  I watched the video a few weeks ago. 我是几周前看的这个视频。
描述感受  I found it on a video-sharing platform. 我是在视频平台上发现它的。
      I watched it at home in the evening. 我是在晚上在家看的。
      The video became popular online. 这个视频当时在网上很火。
      A friend also recommended it to me. 一个朋友也推荐了它。
      The video was about wildlife and nature. 这个视频是关于野生动物和自然的。
      It showed beautiful natural scenery. 它展示了美丽的自然景色。
      The camera work was impressive. 拍摄方式很棒。
      The narrator explained things clearly. 讲解者解释得很清楚。
      I learned many new things from it. 我从中学到了很多新东西。
      I found the video both relaxing and educational. 我觉得这个视频既放松又有教育意义。
      It kept my attention the whole time. 它从头到尾都吸引着我。
      I felt more interested in nature after watching it. 看完后我对自然更感兴趣了。
      The video left a strong impression on me. 这个视频给我留下了深刻印象。
      That is why I think it was very interesting. 这就是我觉得它很有趣的原因。

Sample Answer ：

An interesting video I watched recently was a nature documentary video that I found online a few weeks ago. I
watched it at home one evening after finishing my work. At first, I only planned to watch it for a few minutes,
but the content became so interesting that I ended up watching the entire video.
The video was mainly about wildlife and natural scenery in different parts of the world. It showed forests,
mountains, oceans, and many wild animals living in their natural environment. What impressed me most was
the camera work, because the scenes looked extremely clear and beautiful. The narrator also explained things
in a simple and interesting way, so it was easy to understand even for people who do not know much about
nature.

87
I decided to watch this video because one of my friends recommended it to me. He said it was relaxing and
educational, and he was right. While watching it, I felt calm and completely focused on the scenes. I also
learned many new things about animals and the environment. After watching the video, I became more
interested in nature and wanted to learn more about wildlife protection.
Overall, I think it was a very interesting video because it was not only entertaining, but also meaningful and
informative. It helped me relax while teaching me something valuable at the same time.

Part 3
Question 1: What kind of videos do people in your country like to watch?

 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“娱乐”“学习”“短视频趋势”展开。
Sample Answer:
People in my country enjoy many kinds of videos. Short videos are very popular because they are quick and
entertaining. Many people watch funny clips, food videos, or travel content to relax.
Educational videos are also common, especially among students and young workers. People use them to learn
languages, cooking, or work skills.
So, people usually prefer videos that are interesting, useful, and easy to watch in daily life.

Question 2: Which is more helpful, watching videos or reading books?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以采取平衡观点，但说明各自优势。

Sample Answer:
Both watching videos and reading books are helpful, but they help people in different ways. Videos are easier
to understand quickly because they use pictures and sound. They are useful for learning practical skills or
watching demonstrations.
Books are better for deep thinking and concentration. Reading helps improve imagination, vocabulary, and
understanding of complex ideas.
So, videos are more convenient for quick learning, while books are more helpful for deeper learning and
thinking.

Question 3: What skills can people learn from watching videos?
 解题思路：说明类问题。可以从“实用技能”“学习效率”“兴趣培养”三个角度展开。

Sample Answer:
People can learn many useful skills from watching videos. For example, they can learn cooking, languages, or
computer skills through online tutorials.
Videos are helpful because people can clearly see each step and repeat difficult parts. Watching videos can also
inspire people to try new hobbies or creative activities.
So, videos make learning more visual, convenient, and interesting for many people.

Question 4: Are there any differences between the videos that young people and old people like to watch?
 解题思路：对比类问题。可以从“内容类型”“观看目的”“平台使用”展开。

Sample Answer:
Yes, there are clear differences. Young people often enjoy short videos, games, music, or fashion content
because these videos are fast and entertaining. They usually watch videos on apps and social media platforms.
Older people may prefer news, health programs, cooking videos, or traditional TV shows. They often watch
videos to learn useful information or relax quietly.
So, age, interests, and lifestyle strongly influence the kinds of videos people like to watch.
